In autonomous driving, for instance, a steering command might be internally consistent but decouple from the resulting lateral acceleration. Residual-based detectors can miss such anomalies when flexible sequence models still reconstruct signals plausibly despite altered coordination. We introduce AxonAD, an unsupervised detector that treats multi-head attention query evolution as a short horizon predictable process. A gradient-updated reconstruction pathway is coupled with a history-only predictor that forecasts future query vectors from past context. This is trained via a masked predictor-target objective against an exponential moving average (EMA) target encoder. At inference, reconstruction error is combined with a tail-aggregated query mismatch score, which measures cosine deviation between predicted and target queries on recent timesteps. This dual approach provides sensitivity to structural dependency shifts while retaining amplitude-level detection. On proprietary in-vehicle telemetry with interval annotations and on the TSB-AD multi-variate suite (17 datasets, 180 series) with threshold-free and range-aware metrics, AxonAD improves ranking quality and temporal localization over strong baselines. Ablations confirm that query prediction and combined scoring are the primary drivers of the observed gains. Code is available at the URL https://github.com/iis-esslingen/AxonAD.

</details>


### [41] [SCOPE: Semantic Coreset with Orthogonal Projection Embeddings for Federated learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12976)
*Md Anwar Hossen,Nathan R. Tallent,Luanzheng Guo,Ali Jannesary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOPE：一种用于联邦学习的语义核心集框架，通过正交投影嵌入解决极端类别不平衡问题，实现高效通信和鲁棒收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现需要处理联邦数据集中的极端类别不平衡问题，现有ML方法要么需要不切实际的数据聚合，要么因类别不平衡而失败，现有核心集选择方法依赖局部启发式方法，无法感知全局数据分布且容易产生次优剪枝

Method: SCOPE通过分析潜在空间分布，使用三个评分指标：表示分数（衡量核心类特征的可靠性）、多样性分数（量化正交残差的新颖性）和边界邻近分数（指示与竞争类的相似性）。仅与联邦服务器共享标量指标构建全局共识，动态过滤局部噪声并丢弃冗余样本以对抗全局特征偏斜

Result: SCOPE在保持竞争性全局准确率和鲁棒收敛的同时，实现了128x到512x的上行带宽减少、7.72x的挂钟加速，并减少了本地核心集选择的FLOP和VRAM占用

Conclusion: SCOPE为联邦学习中的极端类别不平衡问题提供了一种有效的核心集解决方案，通过语义分析和正交投影嵌入实现了通信效率和模型性能的良好平衡

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires learning on federated datasets, fed by streams from high-resolution instruments, that have extreme class imbalance. Current ML approaches either require impractical data aggregation or fail due to class imbalance. Existing coreset selection methods rely on local heuristics, making them unaware of the global data landscape and prone to sub-optimal and non-representative pruning. To overcome these challenges, we introduce SCOPE (Semantic Coreset using Orthogonal Projection Embeddings for Federated learning), a coreset framework for federated data that filters anomalies and adaptively prunes redundant data to mitigate long-tail skew. By analyzing the latent space distribution, we score each data point using a representation score that measures the reliability of core class features, a diversity score that quantifies the novelty of orthogonal residuals, and a boundary proximity score that indicates similarity to competing classes. Unlike prior methods, SCOPE shares only scalar metrics with a federated server to construct a global consensus, ensuring communication efficiency. Guided by the global consensus, SCOPE dynamically filters local noise and discards redundant samples to counteract global feature skews. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SCOPE yields competitive global accuracy and robust convergence, all while achieving exceptional efficiency with a 128x to 512x reduction in uplink bandwidth, a 7.72x wall-clock acceleration and reduced FLOP and VRAM footprints for local coreset selection.

</details>


### [42] [Exact Federated Continual Unlearning for Ridge Heads on Frozen Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12977)
*Yijun Quan,Wentai Wu,Giovanni Montana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对冻结基础模型加岭回归头的联邦遗忘方法，通过维护两个加性充分统计量实现精确遗忘，保证与集中式重训练的点对点一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法针对通用深度模型，依赖近似重建或选择性重训练，难以实现精确且高效的遗忘。本文研究冻结基础模型加岭回归头这一实用但未被充分探索的场景，旨在实现精确、高效的联邦遗忘

Method: 将岭回归的最优解分解为两个加性充分统计量，设计基于固定大小消息的通信协议，支持任意流式的添加和删除请求。服务器维护的回归头在精确算术下与每次请求后的集中式重训练点对点一致

Result: 实验在四个基准测试上验证了理论保证：两种变体均与集中式岭回归重训练匹配，相对Frobenius误差在10^-9以内，每个请求的处理速度比集中式重训练快几个数量级

Conclusion: 该方法为冻结基础模型加岭回归头的联邦学习场景提供了精确、高效的遗忘解决方案，具有确定性重训练等价性保证、顺序和分区不变性，以及零KL散度的贝叶斯证明

Abstract: Foundation models are commonly deployed as frozen feature extractors with a small trainable head to adapt to private, user-generated data in federated settings. The ``right to be forgotten'' requires removing the influence of specific samples or users from the trained model on demand. Existing federated unlearning methods target general deep models and rely on approximate reconstruction or selective retraining, making exactness costly or elusive. We study this problem in a practically relevant but under-explored regime: a frozen foundation model with a ridge-regression head. The exact optimum depends on the data only through two additive sufficient statistics, which we turn into a communication protocol supporting an arbitrary stream of \emph{add} and \emph{delete} requests via fixed-size messages. The server maintains a head that is, in exact arithmetic, \emph{pointwise identical} to centralized retraining after every request. We provide deterministic retrain-equivalence guarantees, order and partition invariance, two server-side variants, and a Bayesian certificate of zero KL divergence. Experiments on four benchmarks confirm the guarantees: both variants match centralized ridge retraining to within $10^{-9}$ relative Frobenius error and complete each request at orders-of-

</details>


### [43] [Retrieval-Enhanced Real Estate Appraisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12986)
*Simon Popelier,Matthieu X. B. Sarazin,Maximilien Bohm,Mathieu Gierski,Hanna Mergui,Matthieu Ospici,Adrien Bernhardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种通过学习选择策略而非硬性规定来选择可比房产的方法，显著改进了房地产评估中的销售比较法可比房产选择


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 销售比较法是房地产评估中最流行的方法之一，但在机器学习应用中，现有方法对可比房产的选择策略通常是硬性规定的，而不是通过学习优化的。这限制了评估模型的性能提升潜力。

Method: 提出了一种混合向量-地理检索模块，能够适应不同的数据集，并与估计模块联合优化。该方法学习可比房产的选择策略，而不是强加固定的选择规则。

Result: 在涵盖美国、巴西和法国五个数据集上的评估表明，该方法显著改进了可比房产的选择。使用精心选择的可比房产可以构建需要更少可比房产和更少参数的模型，同时性能接近最先进模型。

Conclusion: 通过学习选择策略而非硬性规定，可以显著改进房地产评估中销售比较法的可比房产选择，从而构建更高效、参数更少的评估模型，同时保持接近最先进的性能。

Abstract: The Sales Comparison Approach (SCA) is one of the most popular when it comes to real estate appraisal. Used as a reference in real estate expertise and as one of the major types of Automatic Valuation Models (AVM), it recently gained popularity within machine learning methods. The performance of models able to use data represented as sets and graphs made it possible to adapt this methodology efficiently, yielding substantial results. SCA relies on taking past transactions (comparables) as references, selected according to their similarity with the target property's sale. In this study, we focus on the selection of these comparables for real estate appraisal. We demonstrate that the selection of comparables used in many state-of-the-art algorithms can be significantly improved by learning a selection policy instead of imposing it. Our method relies on a hybrid vector-geographical retrieval module capable of adapting to different datasets and optimized jointly with an estimation module. We further show that the use of carefully selected comparables makes it possible to build models that require fewer comparables and fewer parameters with performance close to state-of-the-art models. All our evaluations are made on five datasets which span areas in the United States, Brazil, and France.

</details>


### [44] [Dependency-Aware Parallel Decoding via Attention for Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12996)
*Bumjun Kim,Dongjae Jeon,Moongyu Jeon,Albert No*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAPD方法，利用自注意力构建条件依赖图，通过选择图中的独立集实现并行解码，避免同时更新强耦合的token，无需额外模型或重新训练


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)的并行解码困难，因为每个去噪步骤只提供token级的边缘分布，而同时解掩多个token需要考虑token间的依赖关系

Method: DAPD方法：1) 使用自注意力构建条件依赖图，边表示强token交互，非边表示弱依赖；2) 选择图中的独立集；3) 并行解掩选中的token，避免同时更新强耦合的token

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream模型上的实验表明，DAPD在准确率-步骤权衡上优于现有方法，能实现更全局分布的并行更新，更好地利用dLLMs的任意顺序生成能力

Conclusion: DAPD是一种简单、无需训练的并行解码方法，通过依赖图选择独立集实现高效并行解码，改善了dLLMs的准确率-效率权衡

Abstract: Parallel decoding for diffusion LLMs (dLLMs) is difficult because each denoising step provides only token-wise marginal distributions, while unmasking multiple tokens simultaneously requires accounting for inter-token dependencies. We propose Dependency-Aware Parallel Decoding (DAPD), a simple, training-free decoding method that uses self-attention to induce a conditional dependency graph over masked tokens. At each iteration, edges in this graph capture strong token interactions, while non-edges indicate weak dependence. Parallel decoding is then reduced to selecting an independent set on the graph and unmasking the selected tokens in parallel. This avoids co-updating strongly coupled tokens without auxiliary models or retraining. Experiments on LLaDA and Dream show that DAPD improves the accuracy-steps trade-off over existing methods and enables more globally distributed parallel updates that better exploit the any-order generation capability of dLLMs.

</details>


### [45] [Deconstructing the Failure of Ideal Noise Correction: A Three-Pillar Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12997)
*Chen Feng,Zhuo Zhi,Zhao Huang,Jiawei Ge,Ling Xiao,Nicu Sebe,Georgios Tzimiropoulos,Ioannis Patras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文挑战了噪声标签学习中基于转移矩阵方法的传统认知，通过实验证明即使提供完美的转移矩阵，这些方法仍会出现性能崩溃，表明问题根源不在于矩阵估计，而在于更深层的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是检验一个长期假设：基于噪声转移矩阵(T)的统计一致性方法在理论上能收敛到最优分类器，但在实践中常被经验方法超越，传统观点认为这是因为T矩阵估计困难。作者质疑这一假设，想要验证即使有完美的T矩阵，这些方法是否仍会失败。

Method: 在理想化条件下进行实验，为噪声校正方法提供完美的、先知般的转移矩阵（oracle transition matrix）。通过统一分析连接三个层面：宏观收敛状态、微观优化动态以及从噪声标签中可学习内容的信息论极限。

Result: 即使在理想条件下，提供完美的转移矩阵，噪声校正方法在训练过程中仍会出现性能崩溃。这证明失败的根本原因不是T矩阵估计问题，而是更深层的缺陷。

Conclusion: 理想的噪声校正方法失败的原因超出了转移矩阵估计问题，需要从更根本的层面理解其局限性。研究为设计更可靠的噪声标签学习方法提供了具体指导，并建立了连接宏观收敛、微观优化和信息论极限的统一分析框架。

Abstract: Statistically consistent methods based on the noise transition matrix ($T$) offer a theoretically grounded solution to Learning with Noisy Labels (LNL), with guarantees of convergence to the optimal clean-data classifier. In practice, however, these methods are often outperformed by empirical approaches such as sample selection, and this gap is usually attributed to the difficulty of accurately estimating $T$. The common assumption is that, given a perfect $T$, noise-correction methods would recover their theoretical advantage. In this work, we put this longstanding hypothesis to a decisive test. We conduct experiments under idealized conditions, providing correction methods with a perfect, oracle transition matrix. Even under these ideal conditions, we observe that these methods still suffer from performance collapse during training. This compellingly demonstrates that the failure is not fundamentally a $T$-estimation problem, but stems from a more deeply rooted flaw. To explain this behaviour, we provide a unified analysis that links three levels: macroscopic convergence states, microscopic optimisation dynamics, and information-theoretic limits on what can be learned from noisy labels. Together, these results give a formal account of why ideal noise correction fails and offer concrete guidance for designing more reliable methods for learning with noisy labels.

</details>


### [46] [3DTCR: A Physics-Based Generative Framework for Vortex-Following 3D Reconstruction to Improve Tropical Cyclone Intensity Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13049)
*Jun Liu,Xiaohui Zhong,Kai Zheng,Jiarui Li,Yifei Li,Tao Zhou,Wenxu Qian,Shun Dai,Ruian Tie,Yangyang Zhao,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 3DTCR是一个基于物理约束的生成式AI框架，用于三维热带气旋结构重建，结合物理约束与生成式AI效率，在保持轨迹稳定性的同时改进TC内核结构和强度表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带气旋强度预报面临挑战：现有数值和AI天气模型无法充分表示极端TC结构和强度；强度时间序列预报只能输出强度序列而非三维内核精细结构和物理机制；高分辨率数值模拟能捕获这些特征但计算成本高，不适合大规模业务应用。

Method: 提出3DTCR框架，结合物理约束与生成式AI效率，使用条件流匹配(CFM)进行三维TC结构重建。基于6年、3公里分辨率的移动域WRF数据集训练，通过潜在域适应和两阶段迁移学习优化，实现区域自适应涡旋跟踪重建。

Result: 3DTCR在几乎所有预报时效（最长5天）上优于ECMWF高分辨率预报系统(ECMWF-HRES)，相对于其FuXi输入，最大WS10M的RMSE降低了36.5%。该框架缓解了低分辨率目标和过度平滑预报的限制，改进了TC内核结构和强度表示，同时保持轨迹稳定性。

Conclusion: 3DTCR作为一个基于物理约束的生成式框架，能够以较低计算成本高效解析精细尺度结构，为改进TC强度预报提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Tropical cyclone (TC) intensity forecasting remains challenging as current numerical and AI-based weather models fail to satisfactorily represent extreme TC structure and intensity. Although intensity time-series forecasting has achieved significant advances, it outputs intensity sequences rather than the three-dimensional inner-core fine-scale structure and physical mechanisms governing TC evolution. High-resolution numerical simulations can capture these features but remain computationally expensive and inefficient for large-scale operational applications. Here we present 3DTCR, a physics-based generative framework combining physical constraints with generative AI efficiency for 3D TC structure reconstruction. Trained on a six-year, 3-km-resolution moving-domain WRF dataset, 3DTCR enables region-adaptive vortex-following reconstruction using conditional Flow Matching(CFM), optimized via latent domain adaptation and two-stage transfer learning. The framework mitigates limitations imposed by low-resolution targets and over-smoothed forecasts, improving the representation of TC inner-core structure and intensity while maintaining track stability. Results demonstrate that 3DTCR outperforms the ECMWF high-resolution forecasting system (ECMWF-HRES) in TC intensity prediction at nearly all lead times up to 5 days and reduces the RMSE of maximum WS10M by 36.5\% relative to its FuXi inputs. These findings highlight 3DTCR as a physics-based generative framework that efficiently resolves fine-scale structures at lower computational cost, which may offer a promising avenue for improving TC intensity forecasting.

</details>


### [47] [Causal Cellular Context Transfer Learning (C3TL): An Efficient Architecture for Prediction of Unseen Perturbation Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13051)
*Michael Scholkemper,Sach Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 轻量级框架利用生物干预的结构化特性和归纳偏置，仅需广泛可得的批量分子数据即可准确预测扰动效应，无需大型基础模型或专用硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测化学和遗传扰动对定量细胞状态的影响是计算生物学、分子医学和药物发现的核心挑战。虽然已有研究利用大规模单细胞数据和大型基础模型，但这些计算资源和数据集在学术或临床环境中并不总是可访问，限制了实用性。

Method: 提出轻量级框架，利用生物干预的结构化特性和特定归纳偏置/不变性。该方法利用可获得的扰动效应信息实现新情境的泛化，仅需广泛可得的批量分子数据。

Result: 通过将情境特异性扰动效应预测与真实的大规模干预实验进行比较，证明在新情境中能准确预测。该方法与最先进的基础模型竞争，但需要更简单的数据、更小的模型规模和更少的时间。

Conclusion: 通过关注稳健的批量信号和高效架构，证明无需专有硬件或大型模型即可准确预测扰动效应，为生物医学中利用因果学习方法开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Predicting the effects of chemical and genetic perturbations on quantitative cell states is a central challenge in computational biology, molecular medicine and drug discovery. Recent work has leveraged large-scale single-cell data and massive foundation models to address this task. However, such computational resources and extensive datasets are not always accessible in academic or clinical settings, hence limiting utility. Here we propose a lightweight framework for perturbation effect prediction that exploits the structured nature of biological interventions and specific inductive biases/invariances. Our approach leverages available information concerning perturbation effects to allow generalization to novel contexts and requires only widely-available bulk molecular data. Extensive testing, comparing predictions of context-specific perturbation effects against real, large-scale interventional experiments, demonstrates accurate prediction in new contexts. The proposed approach is competitive with SOTA foundation models but requires simpler data, much smaller model sizes and less time. Focusing on robust bulk signals and efficient architectures, we show that accurate prediction of perturbation effects is possible without proprietary hardware or very large models, hence opening up ways to leverage causal learning approaches in biomedicine generally.

</details>


### [48] [Competition-Aware CPC Forecasting with Near-Market Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13059)
*Sebastian Frey,Edoardo Beccari,Maximilian Kranz,Nicolò Alberto Pellizzari,Ali Mete Karaman,Qiwei Han,Maximilian Kaiser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用Google Ads拍卖日志，通过语义、行为和地理信号来近似潜在竞争，以改进汽车租赁市场关键词的每周CPC预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 付费搜索中的每次点击成本（CPC）是拍卖竞争的结果，但单个广告主只能部分观察到竞争格局。需要开发方法来近似潜在竞争，以改善CPC预测的稳定性和准确性。

Method: 使用1811个关键词系列的Google Ads拍卖日志，构建三种竞争信号：1）基于预训练transformer的语义邻域和语义关键词图；2）通过动态时间规整（DTW）对齐CPC轨迹的行为邻域；3）捕捉本地化需求和市场异质性的地理意图协变量。将这些信号作为独立协变量或关系先验集成到时空图预测器中。

Result: 竞争感知增强在业务相关的中长期预测范围内提高了稳定性和误差特性，特别是在竞争机制变化和波动性影响最大的情况下。语义和地理先验提供了可扩展的方法来近似潜在竞争。

Conclusion: 广泛的市场结果覆盖，结合关键词衍生的语义和地理先验，为近似拍卖驱动市场中的潜在竞争和改进CPC预测提供了可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Cost-per-click (CPC) in paid search is a volatile auction outcome generated by a competitive landscape that is only partially observable from any single advertiser's history. Using Google Ads auction logs from a concentrated car-rental market (2021--2023), we forecast weekly CPC for 1,811 keyword series and approximate latent competition through complementary signals derived from keyword text, CPC trajectories, and geographic market structure. We construct (i) semantic neighborhoods and a semantic keyword graph from pretrained transformer-based representations of keyword text, (ii) behavioral neighborhoods via Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) alignment of CPC trajectories, and (iii) geographic-intent covariates capturing localized demand and marketplace heterogeneity. We extensively evaluate these signals both as stand-alone covariates and as relational priors in spatiotemporal graph forecasters, benchmarking them against strong statistical, neural, and time-series foundation-model baselines. Across methods, competition-aware augmentation improves stability and error profiles at business-relevant medium and longer horizons, where competitive regimes shift and volatility is most consequential. The results show that broad market-outcome coverage, combined with keyword-derived semantic and geographic priors, provides a scalable way to approximate latent competition and improve CPC forecasting in auction-driven markets.

</details>


### [49] [GeoChemAD: Benchmarking Unsupervised Geochemical Anomaly Detection for Mineral Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13068)
*Yihao Ding,Yiran Zhang,Chris Gonzalez,Eun-Jung Holden,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoChemAD是一个开源的地球化学异常检测基准数据集，覆盖多个区域和采样条件，并提出了基于Transformer的GeoChemFormer框架，在异常检测准确性和泛化能力上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球化学异常检测研究存在两个关键限制：1) 单区域场景限制了模型泛化能力；2) 专有数据集导致结果无法复现。需要建立一个开放、多区域的基准数据集和可复现的研究框架。

Method: 1) 构建GeoChemAD开源基准数据集，包含8个子集，涵盖不同空间尺度和采样条件；2) 复现并基准测试多种无监督异常检测方法；3) 提出GeoChemFormer框架，通过自监督预训练学习目标元素感知的地球化学表示。

Result: GeoChemFormer在所有8个子集上均表现出优异且稳健的性能，在异常检测准确性和泛化能力方面均优于现有无监督方法，为可复现研究提供了基础。

Conclusion: 提出的GeoChemAD数据集和GeoChemFormer框架为地球化学异常检测领域提供了可复现的研究基础，GeoChemFormer在多个区域和条件下均表现出优越性能，推动了该方向的发展。

Abstract: Geochemical anomaly detection plays a critical role in mineral exploration as deviations from regional geochemical baselines may indicate mineralization. Existing studies suffer from two key limitations: (1) single region scenarios which limit model generalizability; (2) proprietary datasets, which makes result reproduction unattainable. In this work, we introduce \textbf{GeoChemAD}, an open-source benchmark dataset compiled from government-led geological surveys, covering multiple regions, sampling sources, and target elements. The dataset comprises eight subsets representing diverse spatial scales and sampling conditions. To establish strong baselines, we reproduce and benchmark a range of unsupervised anomaly detection methods, including statistical models, generative and transformer-based approaches. Furthermore, we propose \textbf{GeoChemFormer}, a transformer-based framework that leverages self-supervised pretraining to learn target-element-aware geochemical representations for spatial samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoChemFormer consistently achieves superior and robust performance across all eight subsets, outperforming existing unsupervised methods in both anomaly detection accuracy and generalization capability. The proposed dataset and framework provide a foundation for reproducible research and future development in this direction.

</details>


### [50] [Fractals made Practical: Denoising Diffusion as Partitioned Iterated Function Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13069)
*Ann Dooms*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了扩散模型将噪声转化为图像的本质：确定性DDIM反向链实际上是一个分区迭代函数系统(PIFS)，并基于此框架推导出三个可计算的几何量来完全表征去噪动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扩散模型在将噪声转化为图像时的实际工作原理，为扩散模型的时间表、架构和训练目标提供统一的设计语言和理论基础。

Method: 将确定性DDIM反向链建模为分区迭代函数系统(PIFS)，从PIFS结构中推导出三个可计算的几何量：每步收缩阈值L*_t、对角扩展函数f_t(λ)和全局扩展阈值λ**。通过研究PIFS的分形几何，推导出三个最优设计准则。

Result: 证明了扩散模型的二阶段行为：高噪声下的全局上下文组装（通过扩散的跨补丁注意力）和低噪声下的精细细节合成（通过按补丁的抑制释放，按严格方差顺序）。自注意力自然成为PIFS收缩的基本原语。通过离散Moran方程解析确定PIFS吸引子的Kaplan-Yorke维度。

Conclusion: PIFS框架为扩散模型提供了统一的理论基础，解释了多个经验设计选择（余弦时间表偏移、分辨率相关的logSNR偏移、Min-SNR损失加权和Align Your Steps采样）都是几何优化问题的近似解，将理论与实践联系起来。

Abstract: What is a diffusion model actually doing when it turns noise into a photograph?
  We show that the deterministic DDIM reverse chain operates as a Partitioned Iterated Function System (PIFS) and that this framework serves as a unified design language for denoising diffusion model schedules, architectures, and training objectives. From the PIFS structure we derive three computable geometric quantities: a per-step contraction threshold $L^*_t$, a diagonal expansion function $f_t(λ)$ and a global expansion threshold $λ^{**}$. These quantities require no model evaluation and fully characterize the denoising dynamics. They structurally explain the two-regime behavior of diffusion models: global context assembly at high noise via diffuse cross-patch attention and fine-detail synthesis at low noise via patch-by-patch suppression release in strict variance order. Self-attention emerges as the natural primitive for PIFS contraction. The Kaplan-Yorke dimension of the PIFS attractor is determined analytically through a discrete Moran equation on the Lyapunov spectrum.
  Through the study of the fractal geometry of the PIFS, we derive three optimal design criteria and show that four prominent empirical design choices (the cosine schedule offset, resolution-dependent logSNR shift, Min-SNR loss weighting, and Align Your Steps sampling) each arise as approximate solutions to our explicit geometric optimization problems tuning theory into practice.

</details>


### [51] [Breaking the Tuning Barrier: Zero-Hyperparameters Yield Multi-Corner Analysis Via Learned Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13092)
*Wei W. Xing,Kaiqi Huang,Jiazhan Liu,Hong Qiu,Shan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于基础模型的零调优方法，用于多工艺角电路验证，通过上下文学习实现自动化，将验证成本降低10倍以上


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多工艺角电路验证存在组合爆炸问题（O(K×N)），现有方法面临自动化与准确性之间的权衡：简单模型自动化但无法处理非线性电路，先进AI模型准确但需要大量调优时间，形成"调优障碍"

Method: 1) 用预训练基础模型替代工程先验，该模型在数百万回归任务上预训练；2) 通过上下文学习即时适应每个电路，无需调优或重新训练；3) 注意力机制自动识别工艺角间的共享电路物理特性，实现知识迁移；4) 结合自动特征选择器（1152D到48D）

Result: 达到最先进精度（平均MRE低至0.11%），实现零调优，总验证成本降低超过10倍

Conclusion: 通过基础模型和上下文学习打破了电路验证中的"调优障碍"，实现了自动化、高精度且无需调优的多工艺角分析，显著降低了验证成本

Abstract: Yield Multi-Corner Analysis validates circuits across 25+ Process-Voltage-Temperature corners, resulting in a combinatorial simulation cost of $O(K \times N)$ where $K$ denotes corners and $N$ exceeds $10^4$ samples per corner. Existing methods face a fundamental trade-off: simple models achieve automation but fail on nonlinear circuits, while advanced AI models capture complex behaviors but require hours of hyperparameter tuning per design iteration, forming the Tuning Barrier. We break this barrier by replacing engineered priors (i.e., model specifications) with learned priors from a foundation model pre-trained on millions of regression tasks. This model performs in-context learning, instantly adapting to each circuit without tuning or retraining. Its attention mechanism automatically transfers knowledge across corners by identifying shared circuit physics between operating conditions. Combined with an automated feature selector (1152D to 48D), our method matches state-of-the-art accuracy (mean MREs as low as 0.11\%) with zero tuning, reducing total validation cost by over $10\times$.

</details>


### [52] [Representation Learning for Spatiotemporal Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13227)
*Helen Qu,Rudy Morel,Michael McCabe,Alberto Bietti,François Lanusse,Shirley Ho,Yann LeCun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文评估了通用自监督学习方法在物理系统表示学习中的效果，发现潜在空间学习方法（如JEPA）在下游科学任务上优于像素级预测方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法主要关注物理系统的下一帧预测，但这类模拟器训练成本高且存在误差累积问题。作者希望探索更下游的科学任务（如物理参数估计），这些任务能更直接地评估模型表示的物理相关性

Method: 评估多种通用自监督学习方法在物理系统表示学习中的效果，特别比较了潜在空间学习方法（如联合嵌入预测架构JEPA）与像素级预测方法在下游科学任务上的表现

Result: 研究发现并非所有为物理建模设计的方法都能在下游任务上优于通用自监督学习方法，且潜在空间学习方法（如JEPA）在物理参数估计等任务上表现优于像素级预测方法

Conclusion: 对于物理系统的表示学习，潜在空间学习方法比像素级预测方法更适合下游科学任务，这为物理建模提供了新的视角和更有效的表示学习策略

Abstract: Machine learning approaches to spatiotemporal physical systems have primarily focused on next-frame prediction, with the goal of learning an accurate emulator for the system's evolution in time. However, these emulators are computationally expensive to train and are subject to performance pitfalls, such as compounding errors during autoregressive rollout. In this work, we take a different perspective and look at scientific tasks further downstream of predicting the next frame, such as estimation of a system's governing physical parameters. Accuracy on these tasks offers a uniquely quantifiable glimpse into the physical relevance of the representations of these models. We evaluate the effectiveness of general-purpose self-supervised methods in learning physics-grounded representations that are useful for downstream scientific tasks. Surprisingly, we find that not all methods designed for physical modeling outperform generic self-supervised learning methods on these tasks, and methods that learn in the latent space (e.g., joint embedding predictive architectures, or JEPAs) outperform those optimizing pixel-level prediction objectives. Code is available at https://github.com/helenqu/physical-representation-learning.

</details>

### [53] [Are Dependent Types in Set Theory Feasible?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12827)
*Yunsong Yang,Simon Guilloud,Viktor Kunčak*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 将依赖类型系统嵌入到一阶逻辑与集合论中，并在Lisa证明助手中实现验证的类型检查策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将类型作为集合的范式，将依赖函数类型和宇宙层级嵌入到一阶逻辑与集合论中，实现从集合论公理出发的完全验证的自动化推理

Method: 1. 在Lisa证明助手中实现依赖类型到一阶逻辑与塔斯基-格罗滕迪克集合论公理模式的机械化嵌入
2. 开发证明生成的双向类型检查策略，支持部分子类型化
3. 基于集合论公理和一阶逻辑推理规则构建验证框架

Result: 1. 成功实现了依赖类型系统到一阶逻辑与集合论的嵌入
2. 开发了能够计算类型判断证明的验证类型检查策略
3. 展示了支持依赖类型的自动化推理示例，所有推理都从集合论公理和一阶逻辑规则得到完全验证

Conclusion: 该工作展示了如何将依赖类型系统基于集合论和一阶逻辑构建，实现了类型作为集合的范式，支持统一的相等性和替换规则，为依赖类型提供了完全验证的自动化推理基础

Abstract: Following the types-as-sets paradigm, we present a mechanized embedding of dependent function types with a hierarchy of universes into schematic first-order logic with equality, with axiom schemas of Tarski-Grothendieck set theory. We carry this embedding in the Lisa proof assistant. On top of this foundation, we implement a proof-producing bidirectional type-checking tactic to compute proofs for typing judgements, with partial support for subtyping. We present examples showing how our approach enables automated reasoning for dependent types that is fully verified from set-theoretic axioms and deduction rules for schematic first-order logic with equality. Because types are merely sets, the resulting formalism supports equality that applies to all types and values and permits the usual substitution rules.

</details>


### [54] [Delta1 with LLM: symbolic and neural integration for credible and explainable reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12953)
*Yang Xu,Jun Liu,Shuwei Chen,Chris Nugent,Hailing Guo*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端的可解释性构建管道，将基于FTSC的自动定理生成器Delta1与LLMs集成，实现神经符号推理的可解释性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号推理需要结合逻辑的形式严谨性和LLMs的可解释性，现有框架在这方面的融合不足

Method: 开发Delta1自动定理生成器，基于完全三角标准矛盾(FTSC)在多项式时间内确定性地构造最小不可满足子句集和完整定理；LLM层将定理和证明轨迹转化为自然语言解释

Result: 在医疗保健、合规和监管领域的实证研究表明，Delta1与LLM结合能够实现可解释、可审计且与领域对齐的推理

Conclusion: 该工作推动了逻辑、语言和学习的融合，将构造性定理生成定位为神经符号可解释AI的原则性基础

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic reasoning increasingly demands frameworks that unite the formal rigor of logic with the interpretability of large language models (LLMs). We introduce an end to end explainability by construction pipeline integrating the Automated Theorem Generator Delta1 based on the full triangular standard contradiction (FTSC) with LLMs. Delta1 deterministically constructs minimal unsatisfiable clause sets and complete theorems in polynomial time, ensuring both soundness and minimality by construction. The LLM layer verbalizes each theorem and proof trace into coherent natural language explanations and actionable insights. Empirical studies across health care, compliance, and regulatory domains show that Delta1 and LLM enables interpretable, auditable, and domain aligned reasoning. This work advances the convergence of logic, language, and learning, positioning constructive theorem generation as a principled foundation for neuro-symbolic explainable AI.

</details>


### [55] [Support is Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13018)
*Alexander V. Gheorghiu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文将Sandqvist的直觉命题逻辑基扩展语义中的局部支持关系解释为二阶遗传Harrop逻辑程序中的证明搜索，建立了语义与计算之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sandqvist的基扩展语义定义了基于原子基的参数化支持关系，其完备性定理解决了全局问题（哪些公式有效）。本文旨在解决局部问题：在固定基中，支持关系对应什么？

Method: 通过将公式编码为逻辑编程目标，采用延续传递风格解读语义子句，证明固定基中的支持关系等同于二阶遗传Harrop逻辑程序中的证明搜索。

Result: 固定基中的支持关系与二阶遗传Harrop逻辑程序中的证明搜索完全一致，表明基扩展语义中的全称量词不是对已完成总体的域范围量词，而是受标准新鲜性约束的特征变量。

Conclusion: 基扩展语义具有完全构造性和计算透明的解释：支持即证明搜索。这一结果补充了Sandqvist的全局定理，在自身框架内验证了反实在论基础，并为在建模任务中实现该语义开辟了道路。

Abstract: Sandqvist's base-extension semantics for intuitionistic propositional logic defines a support relation parametrised by atomic bases, with validity identified as support in every base. Sandqvist's completeness theorem answers the global question: which formulae are valid? This paper addresses the local question: given a fixed base, what does support in that base correspond to? We show that support in a fixed base coincides with proof-search in a second-order hereditary Harrop logic program, via an encoding of formulae as logic-programming goals. This encoding proceeds by reading the semantic clauses in continuation-passing style, revealing that the universal quantifiers over base extensions and atoms appearing in those clauses are not domain-ranging quantifiers over a completed totality, but eigenvariables governed by a standard freshness discipline. Base-extension semantics thereby admits a fully constructive and computationally transparent interpretation: support is proof-search. The result complements Sandqvist's global theorem with a local correspondence, vindicates the anti-realist foundations of the framework on its own terms, and opens the way for implementing the semantics in modelling tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

### [56] [Push, Press, Slide: Mode-Aware Planar Contact Manipulation via Reduced-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12399)
*Melih Özcan,Ozgur S. Oguz,Umut Orguner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于接触拓扑选择和降阶运动学建模的模式感知框架，用于单臂和双臂平面非抓取操作，通过离散化物理直观模型库简化复杂的接触力学计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面非抓取操作（如推动和按压滑动）在机器人任务中至关重要，但由于混合接触力学、欠驱动性和不对称摩擦限制等挑战，传统方法需要计算昂贵的迭代控制

Method: 提出模式感知框架，将复杂的力矩-速度极限表面力学抽象为离散的物理直观模型库，系统地将各种接触拓扑映射到简单的非完整公式，通过降阶模型进行轨迹生成，并使用直接代数分配器计算所需物体力矩并分配可行的摩擦边界接触力

Result: 在仿真中展示了快速、无需优化的方法在多种单臂和双臂操作任务中的有效性，能够确保长时域可行性

Conclusion: 通过接触拓扑选择和降阶运动学建模，实现了高效、计算简单的平面非抓取操作控制框架，为复杂接触力学问题提供了实用的解决方案

Abstract: Non-prehensile planar manipulation, including pushing and press-and-slide, is critical for diverse robotic tasks, but notoriously challenging due to hybrid contact mechanics, under-actuation, and asymmetric friction limits that traditionally necessitate computationally expensive iterative control. In this paper, we propose a mode-aware framework for planar manipulation with one or two robotic arms based on contact topology selection and reduced-order kinematic modeling. Our core insight is that complex wrench-twist limit surface mechanics can be abstracted into a discrete library of physically intuitive models. We systematically map various single-arm and bimanual contact topologies to simple non-holonomic formulations, e.g. unicycle for simplified press-and-slide motion. By anchoring trajectory generation to these reduced-order models, our framework computes the required object wrench and distributes feasible, friction-bounded contact forces via a direct algebraic allocator. We incorporate manipulator kinematics to ensure long-horizon feasibility and demonstrate our fast, optimization-free approach in simulation across diverse single-arm and bimanual manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [57] [A Learning-Based Approach for Contact Detection, Localization, and Force Estimation of Continuum Manipulators With Integrated OFDR Optical Fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12347)
*Mobina Tavangarifard,Jonathan S. Kacines,Qiyu Li,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于级联学习框架的方法，利用单根分布式OFDR光纤实现连续体机械臂的接触检测、位置定位和力估计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机械臂在微创手术中应用广泛，但其分布式变形特性使得力感知、接触检测、定位和力估计具有挑战性，特别是当相互作用发生在机器人未知弧长位置时

Method: 提出级联学习框架：首先使用梯度提升分类器检测接触，然后使用CNN-FiLM模型预测沿机械臂的空间力分布，从而估计接触位置和相互作用力大小。系统仅使用嵌入机器人一侧的单根分布式OFDR光纤提供密集应变测量

Result: 实验验证表明，在障碍环境中对传感器化肌腱驱动连续体机械臂的测试证明，单根分布式OFDR光纤能够提供足够信息来联合推断接触发生、位置和力

Conclusion: 提出的级联学习框架有效解决了连续体机械臂的接触感知问题，仅需单根分布式光纤即可实现接触检测、定位和力估计，为微创手术中的力反馈提供了可行方案

Abstract: Continuum manipulators (CMs) are widely used in minimally invasive procedures due to their compliant structure and ability to navigate deep and confined anatomical environments. However, their distributed deformation makes force sensing, contact detection, localization, and force estimation challenging, particularly when interactions occur at unknown arc-length locations along the robot. To address this problem, we propose a cascade learning-based framework (CLF) for CMs instrumented with a single distributed Optical Frequency Domain Reflectometry (OFDR) fiber embedded along one side of the robot. The OFDR sensor provides dense strain measurements along the manipulator backbone, capturing strain perturbations caused by external interactions. The proposed CLF first detects contact using a Gradient Boosting classifier and then estimates contact location and interaction force magnitude using a CNN--FiLM model that predicts a spatial force distribution along the manipulator. Experimental validation on a sensorized tendon-driven CM in an obstructed environment demonstrates that a single distributed OFDR fiber provides sufficient information to jointly infer contact occurrence, location, and force in continuum manipulators.

</details>


### [58] [Skill-informed Data-driven Haptic Nudges for High-dimensional Human Motor Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12583)
*Ankur Kamboj,Rajiv Ranganathan,Xiaobo Tan,Vaibhav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出数据驱动的技能感知框架，通过POMDP优化高维运动学习任务中的触觉提示反馈设计，显著加速学习效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维新颖运动学习任务，需要设计有效的触觉提示反馈来加速学习过程。传统启发式方法缺乏理论基础，无法根据学习者技能状态动态调整反馈

Method: 1. 使用输入-输出隐马尔可夫模型(IOHMM)建模人类运动学习的随机动态，解耦潜在技能演化与可观测运动学发射；2. 基于预测模型将触觉提示反馈设计问题表述为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)；3. 推导最小化长期性能成本的最优提示策略，引导学习者进入技能空间的鲁棒区域

Result: 通过30人参与的手部外骨骼任务实验验证：1. POMDP策略组相比启发式反馈组和无反馈组显著加速任务表现；2. 协同分析显示POMDP组更快发现高效的低维运动表征

Conclusion: 提出的数据驱动技能感知框架能够有效设计最优触觉提示反馈，加速高维运动学习，为个性化运动技能训练提供了理论基础和实用方法

Abstract: In this work, we propose a data-driven skill-informed framework to design optimal haptic nudge feedback for high-dimensional novel motor learning tasks. We first model the stochastic dynamics of human motor learning using an Input-Output Hidden Markov Model (IOHMM), which explicitly decouples latent skill evolution from observable kinematic emissions. Leveraging this predictive model, we formulate the haptic nudge feedback design problem as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP). This allows us to derive an optimal nudging policy that minimizes long-term performance cost, implicitly guiding the learner toward robust regions of the skill space. We validated our approach through a human-subject study ($N=30$) using a high-dimensional hand-exoskeleton task. Results demonstrate that participants trained with the POMDP-derived policy exhibited significantly accelerated task performance compared to groups receiving heuristic-based feedback or no feedback. Furthermore, synergy analysis revealed that the POMDP group discovered efficient low-dimensional motor representations more rapidly.

</details>


### [59] [Reinforcement Learning for Elliptical Cylinder Motion Control Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12807)
*Pawel Marczewski,Paulina Superczynska,Jakub Bernat,Szymon Szczesny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了椭圆圆柱体在有限扭矩输入下的控制问题，使用强化学习方法解决，并与基于能量整形的摆起控制器和LQR稳定器的经典两阶段控制器进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于无缆磁控设备等实际应用场景，这些设备由于距离限制只能在有限扭矩下工作。椭圆圆柱体控制问题具有挑战性，是非平凡的控制问题，可作为倒立摆等经典控制问题的基准测试。

Method: 采用强化学习方法解决椭圆圆柱体的有限扭矩控制问题。同时设计了一个经典的两阶段控制器作为基线：第一阶段使用能量整形摆起控制器将系统驱动到目标平衡点附近，第二阶段使用局部线性化模型设计的LQR控制器进行稳定控制。

Result: 研究表明学习控制是可行的，但对于某些特定情况（如向上位置稳定或半转旋转）存在困难，特别是当质量增加或椭圆长短轴比例差异较大时。强化学习策略与经典两阶段控制器在相同限制和参数下进行了对比。

Conclusion: 椭圆圆柱体在有限扭矩下的控制问题可以通过强化学习方法解决，但某些特定情况仍具挑战性。经典的两阶段控制器为欠驱动系统提供了强大且可解释的参考基准，可用于与学习策略进行对比。

Abstract: The control of devices with limited input always bring attention to solve by research due to its difficulty and non-trival solution. For instance, the inverted pendulum is benchmarking problem in control theory and machine learning. In this work, we are focused on the elliptical cylinder and its motion under limited torque. The inspiration of the problem is from untethered magnetic devices, which due to distance have to operate with limited input torque. In this work, the main goal is to define the control problem of elliptic cylinder with limited input torque and solve it by Reinforcement Learning. As a classical baseline, we evaluate a two-stage controller composed of an energy-shaping swing-up law and a local Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) stabilizer around the target equilibrium. The swing-up controller increases the system's mechanical energy to drive the state toward a neighborhood of the desired equilibrium, a linearization of the nonlinear model yields an LQR that regulates the angle and angular-rate states to the target orientation with bounded input. This swing-up + LQR policy is a strong, interpretable reference for underactuated system and serves a point of comparison to the learned policy under identical limits and parameters. The solution shows that the learning is possible however, the different cases like stabilization in upward position or rotating of half turn are very difficult for increasing mass or ellipses with a strongly unequal perimeter ratio.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Motion Imitation: Is Human Motion Data Alone Sufficient to Explain Gait Control and Biomechanics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12408)
*Xinyi Liu,Jangwhan Ahn,Edgar Lobaton,Jennie Si,He Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在基于强化学习的运动模仿学习中，仅匹配运动学无法保证生物力学合理的关节动力学；添加足地接触和接触力作为奖励项能显著改善关节力矩预测，使其更接近逆动力学计算结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着运动模仿学习在人体生物力学和可穿戴机器人领域的应用增长，本研究旨在探究在强化学习框架中，将足地交互测量作为额外奖励项如何影响人体步态运动学和动力学的估计。

Method: 采用基于强化学习的运动模仿学习框架，通过添加足地接触和接触力作为奖励项，与仅使用运动学匹配的方法进行对比，评估其对关节力矩预测的影响。

Result: 仅准确再现前向运动学不能保证生物力学合理的关节动力学；添加足地接触和接触力作为奖励项后，前向行走模拟中的关节力矩预测显著更接近逆动力学计算结果。

Conclusion: 运动学匹配优先的模仿学习方法存在根本局限性；在生物力学和可穿戴机器人协同设计等人体相关研究领域应用模仿学习时，必须包含基于动力学的奖励塑造才能获得物理一致的步态表示。

Abstract: With the growing interest in motion imitation learning (IL) for human biomechanics and wearable robotics, this study investigates how additional foot-ground interaction measures, used as reward terms, affect human gait kinematics and kinetics estimation within a reinforcement learning-based IL framework. Results indicate that accurate reproduction of forward kinematics alone does not ensure biomechanically plausible joint kinetics. Adding foot-ground contacts and contact forces to the IL reward terms enables the prediction of joint moments in forward walking simulation, which are significantly closer to those computed by inverse dynamics. This finding highlights a fundamental limitation of motion-only IL approaches, which may prioritize kinematics matching over physical consistency. Incorporating kinetic constraints, particularly ground reaction force and center of pressure information, significantly enhances the realism of internal and external kinetics. These findings suggest that, when imitation learning is applied to human-related research domains such as biomechanics and wearable robot co-design, kinetics-based reward shaping is necessary to achieve physically consistent gait representations.

</details>


### [61] [Predictive and adaptive maps for long-term visual navigation in changing environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12460)
*Lucie Halodova,Eliska Dvorakova,Filip Majer,Tomas Vintr,Oscar Martinez Mozos,Feras Dayoub,Tomas Krajnik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 比较不同地图管理技术在变化环境中长期视觉导航的性能，发现建模环境周期性变化并预测特征可见性的策略优于不显式建模时间演化的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在变化环境中实现可靠的长期视觉导航需要地图管理系统能够适应环境外观变化，包括选择有用特征、移除过时特征、添加新特征。

Method: 提出多种地图管理策略，在长期教学-重复导航任务中评估机器人定位精度，实验持续三个月。

Result: 建模环境外观周期性变化并预测特定时间和位置特征可见性的策略，优于不显式建模时间演化的策略。

Conclusion: 对于长期视觉导航，显式建模环境变化的时间演化对地图管理至关重要，周期性变化建模能显著提升导航性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we compare different map management techniques for long-term visual navigation in changing environments. In this scenario, the navigation system needs to continuously update and refine its feature map in order to adapt to the environment appearance change. To achieve reliable long-term navigation, the map management techniques have to (i) select features useful for the current navigation task, (ii) remove features that are obsolete, (iii) and add new features from the current camera view to the map. We propose several map management strategies and evaluate their performance with regard to the robot localisation accuracy in long-term teach-and-repeat navigation. Our experiments, performed over three months, indicate that strategies which model cyclic changes of the environment appearance and predict which features are going to be visible at a particular time and location, outperform strategies which do not explicitly model the temporal evolution of the changes.

</details>


### [62] [Conflict Mitigation in Shared Environments using Flow-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12736)
*Lukas Heuer,Yufei Zhu,Luigi Palmieri,Andrey Rudenko,Anna Mannucci,Sven Koenig,Martin Magnusson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FA-MAPF框架将不可控智能体的学习运动模式集成到集中式MAPF算法中，在多机器人系统中减少与动态不可控智能体的冲突，最高可达55%，同时保持任务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人系统与动态不可控智能体共存的环境中，现有研究主要关注延迟情况下的MAPF解决方案完整性，而缺乏利用额外环境信息来提升解决方案质量的方法。个体机器人操作可能因与不可控智能体的意外冲突而延迟。

Method: 提出Flow-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding (FA-MAPF)框架，将学习到的不可控智能体运动模式集成到集中式MAPF算法中。该方法利用环境信息来优化路径规划。

Result: 在具有模拟不可控智能体的多样化基准地图和具有记录人类轨迹的真实世界地图上的评估表明，FA-MAPF相比最先进的基线方法能持续减少与不可控智能体的冲突，最高可达55%，且不损害任务效率。

Conclusion: FA-MAPF框架通过集成不可控智能体的学习运动模式，有效提升了多机器人系统在动态共享环境中的路径规划质量，显著减少了冲突，同时保持了任务效率。

Abstract: Deploying multi-robot systems in environments shared with dynamic and uncontrollable agents presents significant challenges, especially for large robot fleets. In such environments, individual robot operations can be delayed due to unforeseen conflicts with uncontrollable agents. While existing research primarily focuses on preserving the completeness of Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) solutions considering delays, there is limited emphasis on utilizing additional environmental information to enhance solution quality in the presence of other dynamic agents. To this end, we propose Flow-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding (FA-MAPF), a novel framework that integrates learned motion patterns of uncontrollable agents into centralized MAPF algorithms. Our evaluation, conducted on a diverse set of benchmark maps with simulated uncontrollable agents and on a real-world map with recorded human trajectories, demonstrates the effectiveness of FA-MAPF compared to state-of-the-art baselines. The experimental results show that FA-MAPF can consistently reduce conflicts with uncontrollable agents, up to 55%, without compromising task efficiency.

</details>


### [63] [From Passive Monitoring to Active Defence: Resilient Control of Manipulators Under Cyberattacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13003)
*Gabriele Gualandi,Alessandro V. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出针对冗余机械臂的主动防御架构，通过基于异常评分的控制衰减机制抵御隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击，同时保证闭环稳定性和名义性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息物理机器人系统易受虚假数据注入攻击（FDIAs），攻击者能通过操纵传感器信号绕过基于残差的被动异常检测器（如卡方检验），导致末端执行器显著偏差而不触发警报。现有被动监控方法不足以应对此类隐蔽攻击。

Method: 建立包含反馈线性化机械臂、稳态卡尔曼滤波器和卡方异常检测器的闭环模型。在被动监控层基础上，提出主动控制级防御：通过基于新型驱动投影、无测量状态预测器生成的异常评分的单调函数衰减控制输入。从攻击者角度，推导出计算一步最优隐蔽攻击的凸QCQP问题。

Result: 所提设计为名义驱动损失提供概率保证并保持闭环稳定性。在6自由度平面机械臂上的仿真表明，该防御能显著减少攻击引起的末端执行器偏差，同时在无攻击时保持名义任务性能。

Conclusion: 将冗余机械臂的架构从被动监控推进到主动防御，提出的控制级防御能有效抵御隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击，在保证系统稳定性的同时最小化攻击影响。

Abstract: Cyber-physical robotic systems are vulnerable to false data injection attacks (FDIAs), in which an adversary corrupts sensor signals while evading residual-based passive anomaly detectors such as the chi-squared test. Such stealthy attacks can induce substantial end-effector deviations without triggering alarms. This paper studies the resilience of redundant manipulators to stealthy FDIAs and advances the architecture from passive monitoring to active defence. We formulate a closed-loop model comprising a feedback-linearized manipulator, a steady-state Kalman filter, and a chi-squared-based anomaly detector. Building on this passive monitoring layer, we propose an active control-level defence that attenuates the control input through a monotone function of an anomaly score generated by a novel actuation-projected, measurement-free state predictor. The proposed design provides probabilistic guarantees on nominal actuation loss and preserves closed-loop stability. From the attacker perspective, we derive a convex QCQP for computing one-step optimal stealthy attacks. Simulations on a 6-DOF planar manipulator show that the proposed defence significantly reduces attack-induced end-effector deviation while preserving nominal task performance in the absence of attacks.

</details>


### [64] [One-Step Flow Policy: Self-Distillation for Fast Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12480)
*Shaolong Li,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出One-Step Flow Policy (OFP)，一种无需预训练教师模型的自我蒸馏框架，实现单步动作生成，解决生成流和扩散模型因迭代采样导致的高延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成流和扩散模型能提供连续多模态动作分布，但依赖迭代采样导致严重推理延迟，降低控制频率并损害时间敏感操作性能。

Method: OFP框架包含：1) 自一致性损失确保时间间隔内连贯传输；2) 自引导正则化使预测向专家模式高密度区域锐化；3) 热启动机制利用时间动作相关性最小化生成传输距离。

Result: 在56个多样化模拟操作任务中，单步OFP达到最先进结果，优于100步扩散和流策略，动作生成加速超过100倍。在RoboTwin 2.0的π0.5模型中集成OFP，单步OFP超越原始10步策略。

Conclusion: OFP为高精度低延迟机器人控制提供了实用、可扩展的解决方案，通过从零开始的自我蒸馏实现单步高保真动作生成。

Abstract: Generative flow and diffusion models provide the continuous, multimodal action distributions needed for high-precision robotic policies. However, their reliance on iterative sampling introduces severe inference latency, degrading control frequency and harming performance in time-sensitive manipulation. To address this problem, we propose the One-Step Flow Policy (OFP), a from-scratch self-distillation framework for high-fidelity, single-step action generation without a pre-trained teacher. OFP unifies a self-consistency loss to enforce coherent transport across time intervals, and a self-guided regularization to sharpen predictions toward high-density expert modes. In addition, a warm-start mechanism leverages temporal action correlations to minimize the generative transport distance. Evaluations across 56 diverse simulated manipulation tasks demonstrate that a one-step OFP achieves state-of-the-art results, outperforming 100-step diffusion and flow policies while accelerating action generation by over $100\times$. We further integrate OFP into the $π_{0.5}$ model on RoboTwin 2.0, where one-step OFP surpasses the original 10-step policy. These results establish OFP as a practical, scalable solution for highly accurate and low-latency robot control.

</details>


### [65] [COAD: Constant-Time Planning for Continuous Goal Manipulation with Compressed Library and Online Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12488)
*Adil Shiyas,Zhuoyun Zhong,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COAD框架通过任务覆盖区域离散化连续任务空间，仅求解代表性根问题构建压缩运动库，实现连续目标参数化任务空间的常数时间规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人操作任务中，机器人需要重复解决运动规划问题，这些问题主要差异在于目标物体及其关联障碍物的位置，而周围工作空间保持不变。现有方法缺乏覆盖连续任务空间的保证，且需要存储大量解决方案库

Method: COAD将连续任务空间离散化为有限数量的任务覆盖区域，仅求解代表性根问题构建压缩库，其他问题通过从根解决方案快速适应处理。查询时系统在常数时间内检索根运动，并使用线性插值、动态运动基元或简单轨迹优化等轻量级适应模块将其适应到期望目标

Result: 在多种机械臂和环境的仿真及实际实验中，COAD实现了运动库的显著压缩，同时保持高成功率，查询时间达到亚毫秒级别，在效率和路径质量方面均优于基线方法

Conclusion: COAD框架通过任务覆盖区域离散化和压缩库构建，为连续目标参数化任务空间提供了常数时间规划能力，在保持高性能的同时大幅减少了存储需求

Abstract: In many robotic manipulation tasks, the robot repeatedly solves motion-planning problems that differ mainly in the location of the goal object and its associated obstacle, while the surrounding workspace remains fixed. Prior works have shown that leveraging experience and offline computation can accelerate repeated planning queries, but they lack guarantees of covering the continuous task space and require storing large libraries of solutions. In this work, we present COAD, a framework that provides constant-time planning over a continuous goal-parameterized task space. COAD discretizes the continuous task space into finitely many Task Coverage Regions. Instead of planning and storing solutions for every region offline, it constructs a compressed library by only solving representative root problems. Other problems are handled through fast adaptation from these root solutions. At query time, the system retrieves a root motion in constant time and adapts it to the desired goal using lightweight adaptation modules such as linear interpolation, Dynamic Movement Primitives, or simple trajectory optimization. We evaluate the framework on various manipulators and environments in simulation and the real world, showing that COAD achieves substantial compression of the motion library while maintaining high success rates and sub-millisecond-level queries, outperforming baseline methods in both efficiency and path quality. The source code is available at https://github.com/elpis-lab/CoAd.

</details>


### [66] [Robots that redesign themselves through kinematic self-destruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12505)
*Chen Yu,Sam Kriegman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人通过运动学自毁在生命周期中主动参与自身设计，从随机组装的身体开始，使用本体感觉反馈识别并移除阻碍运动的冗余关节，实现更有效的运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人都是预先设计好的，缺乏在部署后自我调整和优化的能力。本研究旨在探索机器人能否在生命周期中主动参与自身设计，通过自我调整来适应环境并提高性能

Method: 使用单一自回归序列模型作为通用控制器，在模拟中学习何时以及如何通过运动学自毁简化机器人身体结构。机器人从随机组装的身体开始，仅使用本体感觉反馈识别阻碍运动的冗余关节，然后通过剧烈摆动使其断裂脱落

Result: 优化后的策略成功转移到现实世界，并能泛化到未见过的运动学树结构。通过自毁移除冗余关节的机器人比随机移除关节或不能移除任何关节的等效策略产生更有效的前进运动

Conclusion: 自设计机器人在某些情况下可能比预先设计的机器人更成功，运动学自毁虽然具有还原性和不可逆性，但可以为广泛类型的机器人提供一种通用的自适应策略

Abstract: Every robot built to date was predesigned by an external process, prior to deployment. Here we show a robot that actively participates in its own design during its lifetime. Starting from a randomly assembled body, and using only proprioceptive feedback, the robot dynamically ``sculpts'' itself into a new design through kinematic self-destruction: identifying redundant links within its body that inhibit its locomotion, and then thrashing those links against the surface until they break at the joint and fall off the body. It does so using a single autoregressive sequence model, a universal controller that learns in simulation when and how to simplify a robot's body through self-destruction and then adaptively controls the reduced morphology. The optimized policy successfully transfers to reality and generalizes to previously unseen kinematic trees, generating forward locomotion that is more effective than otherwise equivalent policies that randomly remove links or cannot remove any. This suggests that self-designing robots may be more successful than predesigned robots in some cases, and that kinematic self-destruction, though reductive and irreversible, could provide a general adaptive strategy for a wide range of robots.

</details>


### [67] [Red-Teaming Vision-Language-Action Models via Quality Diversity Prompt Generation for Robust Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12510)
*Siddharth Srikanth,Freddie Liang,Sophie Hsu,Varun Bhatt,Shihan Zhao,Henry Chen,Bryon Tjanaka,Minjune Hwang,Akanksha Saran,Daniel Seita,Aaquib Tabrez,Stefanos Nikolaidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Q-DIG是一种通过质量多样性(QD)技术与视觉语言模型(VLM)结合的方法，用于生成多样化的对抗性指令，以识别和改善视觉语言动作(VLA)模型的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人系统中的性能对语言指令的精确措辞高度敏感，且难以预测其失败情况。需要提高VLA对不同措辞的鲁棒性。

Method: Q-DIG整合质量多样性(QD)技术与视觉语言模型(VLM)，通过可扩展地识别多样化的自然语言任务描述来执行红队测试，这些描述能引发失败但仍保持任务相关性。

Result: 在多个仿真基准测试中，Q-DIG相比基线方法发现了更多样化和有意义的失败模式；在生成的指令上微调VLA提高了任务成功率；用户研究显示Q-DIG生成的提示更自然、更接近人类表达；真实世界评估结果与仿真一致。

Conclusion: Q-DIG是识别VLA模型脆弱性并提高其鲁棒性的有前景方法，通过生成的指令进行微调可进一步提高对未见指令的成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have significant potential to enable general-purpose robotic systems for a range of vision-language tasks. However, the performance of VLA-based robots is highly sensitive to the precise wording of language instructions, and it remains difficult to predict when such robots will fail. To improve the robustness of VLAs to different wordings, we present Q-DIG (Quality Diversity for Diverse Instruction Generation), which performs red-teaming by scalably identifying diverse natural language task descriptions that induce failures while remaining task-relevant. Q-DIG integrates Quality Diversity (QD) techniques with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate a broad spectrum of adversarial instructions that expose meaningful vulnerabilities in VLA behavior. Our results across multiple simulation benchmarks show that Q-DIG finds more diverse and meaningful failure modes compared to baseline methods, and that fine-tuning VLAs on the generated instructions improves task success rates. Furthermore, results from a user study highlight that Q-DIG generates prompts judged to be more natural and human-like than those from baselines. Finally, real-world evaluations of Q-DIG prompts show results consistent with simulation, and fine-tuning VLAs on the generated prompts further success rates on unseen instructions. Together, these findings suggest that Q-DIG is a promising approach for identifying vulnerabilities and improving the robustness of VLA-based robots. Our anonymous project website is at qdigvla.github.io.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Dense Futures: World Models as Structured Planners for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12553)
*Minghao Jin,Mozheng Liao,Mingfei Han,Zhihui Li,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StructVLA将生成式世界模型重构为显式结构化规划器，通过预测稀疏的物理意义结构化帧而非密集视觉预测，解决长时程规划漂移问题，实现视觉规划与运动控制的更好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的VLA架构存在两个主要问题：1）密集未来预测引入视觉冗余和误差累积，导致长时程规划漂移；2）稀疏方法通常使用高级语义子任务或隐式潜在状态表示，缺乏显式运动学基础，导致规划与底层执行对齐不足。

Method: StructVLA通过两阶段训练范式实现：1）世界模型首先训练预测稀疏的物理意义结构化帧，这些帧源自内在运动学线索（如夹爪状态转换和运动学转折点）；2）随后优化模型将结构化前瞻映射到低级动作。采用统一的离散标记词汇表，结构化帧捕捉与任务进展密切相关的时空里程碑。

Result: StructVLA在SimperEnv-WidowX上达到75.0%的平均成功率，在LIBERO上达到94.8%的平均成功率。真实世界部署进一步展示了在基础拾放任务和复杂长时程任务上的可靠任务完成和鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: StructVLA通过显式结构化规划框架有效解决了视觉冗余和规划漂移问题，提供了清晰的物理指导，成功桥接了视觉规划与运动控制，在仿真和真实世界任务中均表现出色。

Abstract: Recent world-model-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures have improved robotic manipulation through predictive visual foresight. However, dense future prediction introduces visual redundancy and accumulates errors, causing long-horizon plan drift. Meanwhile, recent sparse methods typically represent visual foresight using high-level semantic subtasks or implicit latent states. These representations often lack explicit kinematic grounding, weakening the alignment between planning and low-level execution. To address this, we propose StructVLA, which reformulates a generative world model into an explicit structured planner for reliable control. Instead of dense rollouts or semantic goals, StructVLA predicts sparse, physically meaningful structured frames. Derived from intrinsic kinematic cues (e.g., gripper transitions and kinematic turning points), these frames capture spatiotemporal milestones closely aligned with task progress. We implement this approach through a two-stage training paradigm with a unified discrete token vocabulary: the world model is first trained to predict structured frames and subsequently optimized to map the structured foresight into low-level actions. This approach provides clear physical guidance and bridges visual planning and motion control. In our experiments, StructVLA achieves strong average success rates of 75.0% on SimplerEnv-WidowX and 94.8% on LIBERO. Real-world deployments further demonstrate reliable task completion and robust generalization across both basic pick-and-place and complex long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [69] [From Woofs to Words: Towards Intelligent Robotic Guide Dogs with Verbal Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12574)
*Yohei Hayamizu,David DeFazio,Hrudayangam Mehta,Zainab Altaweel,Jacqueline Choe,Chao Lin,Jake Juettner,Furui Xiao,Jeremy Blackburn,Shiqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发用于机器人导盲犬的新型对话系统，利用LLM将导航计划和场景信息语言化，实现人机协作决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助机器人领域需要关注残障人士福祉，机器人导盲犬帮助视障人士避障导航。现有对话系统无法直接应用于移动机器人，面临动态环境语言接地和提升人类操作者空间感知的新挑战

Method: 开发基于LLM的新型对话系统，将导航计划和场景信息语言化，支持人机团队协作决策。通过人类研究评估不同语言化策略，通过仿真研究评估导航任务的效率和准确性

Result: 通过人类研究评估了不同语言化策略的效果，通过仿真研究验证了导航任务的效率和准确性

Conclusion: 该研究开发了机器人导盲犬的新型对话系统，通过语言化导航计划和场景信息，解决了动态环境语言接地和空间感知问题，实现了人机协作决策

Abstract: Assistive robotics is an important subarea of robotics that focuses on the well-being of people with disabilities. A robotic guide dog is an assistive quadruped robot that helps visually impaired people in obstacle avoidance and navigation. Enabling language capabilities for robotic guide dogs goes beyond naively adding an existing dialog system onto a mobile robot. The novel challenges include grounding language in the dynamically changing environment and improving spatial awareness for the human handler. To address those challenges, we develop a novel dialog system for robotic guide dogs that uses LLMs to verbalize both navigational plans and scenes. The goal is to enable verbal communication for collaborative decision-making within the handler-robot team. In experiments, we conducted a human study to evaluate different verbalization strategies and a simulation study to assess the efficiency and accuracy in navigation tasks.

</details>


### [70] [CarPLAN: Context-Adaptive and Robust Planning with Dynamic Scene Awareness for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12607)
*Junyong Yun,Jungho Kim,ByungHyun Lee,Dongyoung Lee,Sehwan Choi,Seunghyeop Nam,Kichun Jo,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CarPLAN是一个基于模仿学习的运动规划框架，通过位移感知预测编码增强空间感知能力，并利用上下文自适应多专家解码器实现动态环境中的自适应规划，在nuPlan和Waymax基准测试中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模仿学习的自动驾驶运动规划需要捕捉真实世界驾驶数据中的复杂驾驶上下文，并实现上下文自适应决策，而不仅仅是模仿专家轨迹。现有方法在这方面存在不足，需要增强对驾驶上下文的理解和自适应规划能力。

Method: 提出CarPLAN框架：1) 位移感知预测编码(DPE)，通过预测自动驾驶车辆与周围场景元素之间的未来位移向量来增强空间感知；2) 上下文自适应多专家解码器(CMD)，基于混合专家框架，根据场景结构动态选择最合适的专家解码器；3) 在标准模仿损失基础上增加位移预测误差损失项。

Result: 在nuPlan基准测试中，CarPLAN在所有闭环仿真指标上达到最先进性能，特别是在Test14-Hard等挑战性场景中表现出鲁棒性能。在Waymax基准测试上的额外实验进一步证明了其在不同基准设置下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CarPLAN通过增强驾驶上下文理解和自适应规划能力，显著提升了基于模仿学习的运动规划性能，为复杂动态环境中的自动驾驶提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) is widely used for motion planning in autonomous driving due to its data efficiency and access to real-world driving data. For safe and robust real-world driving, IL-based planning requires capturing the complex driving contexts inherent in real-world data and enabling context-adaptive decision-making, rather than relying solely on expert trajectory imitation. In this paper, we propose CarPLAN, a novel IL-based motion planning framework that explicitly enhances driving context understanding and enables adaptive planning across diverse traffic scenarios. Our contributions are twofold: We introduce Displacement-Aware Predictive Encoding (DPE) to improve the model's spatial awareness by predicting future displacement vectors between the Autonomous Vehicle (AV) and surrounding scene elements. This allows the planner to account for relational spacing when generating trajectories. In addition to the standard imitation loss, we incorporate an augmented loss term that captures displacement prediction errors, ensuring planning decisions consider relative distances from other agents. To improve the model's ability to handle diverse driving contexts, we propose Context-Adaptive Multi-Expert Decoder (CMD), which leverages the Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework. CMD dynamically selects the most suitable expert decoders based on scene structure at each Transformer layer, enabling adaptive and context-aware planning in dynamic environments. We evaluate CarPLAN on the nuPlan benchmark and demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all closed-loop simulation metrics. In particular, CarPLAN exhibits robust performance on challenging scenarios such as Test14-Hard, validating its effectiveness in complex driving conditions. Additional experiments on the Waymax benchmark further demonstrate its generalization capability across different benchmark settings.

</details>


### [71] [Autonomous Integration and Improvement of Robotic Assembly using Skill Graph Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12649)
*Peiqi Yu,Philip Huang,Chaitanya Chawla,Guanya Shi,Jiaoyang Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于技能图表示的机器人装配系统自主集成与持续改进框架，通过语义级规划和闭环学习实现自适应可重用装配系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人装配系统需要大量人工工程来集成新任务、适应新环境和随时间改进性能，缺乏自适应和可重用性

Method: 使用技能图表示，将机器人能力组织为基于动词的技能，明确链接语义描述（动词和名词）与可执行策略、前置条件、后置条件和评估器，支持语义级规划和系统数据收集

Result: 技能图框架统一了系统配置、执行、评估和学习，为自适应可重用机器人装配系统提供了可扩展路径，代码已开源

Conclusion: 技能图表示能够实现机器人装配系统的快速集成和持续改进，将语义级规划与机器人控制器和感知模块的执行相结合，支持迭代式技能优化

Abstract: Robotic assembly systems traditionally require substantial manual engineering effort to integrate new tasks, adapt to new environments, and improve performance over time. This paper presents a framework for autonomous integration and continuous improvement of robotic assembly systems based on Skill Graph representations. A Skill Graph organizes robot capabilities as verb-based skills, explicitly linking semantic descriptions (verbs and nouns) with executable policies, pre-conditions, post-conditions, and evaluators. We show how Skill Graphs enable rapid system integration by supporting semantic-level planning over skills, while simultaneously grounding execution through well-defined interfaces to robot controllers and perception modules. After initial deployment, the same Skill Graph structure supports systematic data collection and closed-loop performance improvement, enabling iterative refinement of skills and their composition. We demonstrate how this approach unifies system configuration, execution, evaluation, and learning within a single representation, providing a scalable pathway toward adaptive and reusable robotic assembly systems. The code is at https://github.com/intelligent-control-lab/AIDF.

</details>


### [72] [TacVLA: Contact-Aware Tactile Fusion for Robust Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12665)
*Kaidi Zhang,Heng Zhang,Zhengtong Xu,Zhiyuan Zhang,Md Rakibul Islam Prince,Xiang Li,Xiaojing Han,Yuhao Zhou,Arash Ajoudani,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacVLA：通过融入触觉模态和接触感知门控机制增强VLA模型在精细操作任务中的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在视觉遮挡、精细操作和物理接触任务中表现不佳，需要引入触觉模态来增强精细操作能力

Method: 提出TacVLA模型，在基于Transformer的策略中融入触觉模态，引入接触感知门控机制，仅在检测到接触时激活触觉令牌，实现自适应多模态融合，避免无关触觉干扰

Result: 在约束锁定拆卸、箱内拾取和鲁棒性评估实验中，模型性能显著优于基线：拆卸任务平均提升20%成功率，箱内拾取提升60%，视觉遮挡场景下性能提升2.1倍

Conclusion: TacVLA通过有效融合视觉、语言和触觉模态，显著增强了VLA模型在接触密集型交互任务中的精细操作能力

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated significant advantages in robotic manipulation. However, their reliance on vision and language often leads to suboptimal performance in tasks involving visual occlusion, fine-grained manipulation, and physical contact. To address these challenges, we propose TacVLA, a fine-tuned VLA model by incorporating tactile modalities into the transformer-based policy to enhance fine-grained manipulation capabilities. Specifically, we introduce a contact-aware gating mechanism that selectively activates tactile tokens only when contact is detected, enabling adaptive multimodal fusion while avoiding irrelevant tactile interference. The fused visual, language, and tactile tokens are jointly processed within the transformer architecture to strengthen cross-modal grounding during contact-rich interaction. Extensive experiments on constraint-locked disassembly, in-box picking and robustness evaluations demonstrate that our model outperforms baselines, improving the performance by averaging 20% success rate in disassembly, 60% in in-box picking and 2.1x improvement in scenarios with visual occlusion. Videos are available at https://sites.google.com/view/tacvla and code will be released.

</details>


### [73] [Learning Athletic Humanoid Tennis Skills from Imperfect Human Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12686)
*Zhikai Zhang,Haofei Lu,Yunrui Lian,Ziqing Chen,Yun Liu,Chenghuai Lin,Han Xue,Zicheng Zeng,Zekun Qi,Shaolin Zheng,Qing Luan,Jingbo Wang,Junliang Xing,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LATENT系统从非完美的人类网球动作数据中学习仿人机器人的网球技能，实现了稳定的多拍对打能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机器人难以再现人类运动员在网球中的高动态技能，主要原因是缺乏完美的仿人动作数据或网球场景中的人类运动学数据作为参考

Method: 使用非完美的人类运动片段数据（仅包含网球基本技能而非完整比赛序列），通过校正和组合学习仿人机器人策略，并设计了鲁棒的仿真到现实迁移方案

Result: 策略能在广泛条件下稳定击打来球并回击到目标位置，同时保持自然运动风格，在Unitree G1仿人机器人上实现了与人类玩家的稳定多拍对打

Conclusion: LATENT系统证明即使使用非完美的人类运动数据，也能通过学习、校正和组合，使仿人机器人掌握复杂的网球技能并实现稳定的现实部署

Abstract: Human athletes demonstrate versatile and highly-dynamic tennis skills to successfully conduct competitive rallies with a high-speed tennis ball. However, reproducing such behaviors on humanoid robots is difficult, partially due to the lack of perfect humanoid action data or human kinematic motion data in tennis scenarios as reference. In this work, we propose LATENT, a system that Learns Athletic humanoid TEnnis skills from imperfect human motioN daTa. The imperfect human motion data consist only of motion fragments that capture the primitive skills used when playing tennis rather than precise and complete human-tennis motion sequences from real-world tennis matches, thereby significantly reducing the difficulty of data collection. Our key insight is that, despite being imperfect, such quasi-realistic data still provide priors about human primitive skills in tennis scenarios. With further correction and composition, we learn a humanoid policy that can consistently strike incoming balls under a wide range of conditions and return them to target locations, while preserving natural motion styles. We also propose a series of designs for robust sim-to-real transfer and deploy our policy on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot. Our method achieves surprising results in the real world and can stably sustain multi-shot rallies with human players. Project page: https://zzk273.github.io/LATENT/

</details>


### [74] [Altered Thoughts, Altered Actions: Probing Chain-of-Thought Vulnerabilities in VLA Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12717)
*Tuan Duong Trinh,Naveed Akhtar,Basim Azam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现VLA模型中的思维链推理存在安全漏洞：仅通过替换中间推理文本中的物体名称就能显著降低机器人任务成功率，而更复杂的语义攻击反而影响有限，表明动作解码器主要依赖实体引用完整性而非推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型广泛采用思维链推理，在生成动作指令前先生成自然语言计划。这种推理模块与动作解码器之间的内部文本通道尚未受到对抗性攻击的审视。研究旨在探究动作解码器实际依赖推理文本的哪些属性，以及仅针对推理轨迹的针对性破坏是否能在保持输入完整的情况下降低机器人物理任务性能。

Method: 设计了七种文本破坏方法，分为三个攻击层级：盲噪声攻击、机械语义攻击和LLM自适应攻击。在40个LIBERO桌面操作任务上对最先进的推理VLA模型进行测试。同时使用非推理VLA模型作为跨架构对照，以验证漏洞的专属性。

Result: 结果显示出显著的不对称性：替换推理文本中的物体名称使总体成功率降低8.3个百分点，在目标条件任务中达到-19.3pp，个别任务中甚至达到-45pp。而句子重排序、空间方向反转、令牌噪声以及70B参数的LLM生成的看似合理但错误的计划影响都微乎其微（在±4pp内）。这表明动作解码器依赖实体引用完整性而非推理质量或顺序结构。值得注意的是，复杂的基于LLM的攻击者表现不如简单的机械物体名称替换，因为保持合理性无意中保留了解码器所需的实体接地结构。

Conclusion: 推理增强模型的内部推理轨迹是一个独特且隐蔽的威胁向量，对输入验证防御不可见。动作解码器主要依赖实体引用完整性而非推理质量，这为VLA模型的安全性设计提供了重要启示：需要保护中间推理文本中的实体引用，而不仅仅是确保语义合理性。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly adopt chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, generating a natural-language plan before decoding motor commands. This internal text channel between the reasoning module and the action decoder has received no adversarial scrutiny. We ask: which properties of this intermediate plan does the action decoder actually rely on, and can targeted corruption of the reasoning trace alone -- with all inputs left intact -- degrade a robot's physical task performance? We design a taxonomy of seven text corruptions organized into three attacker tiers (blind noise, mechanical-semantic, and LLM-adaptive) and apply them to a state-of-the-art reasoning VLA across 40 LIBERO tabletop manipulation tasks. Our results reveal a striking asymmetry: substituting object names in the reasoning trace reduces overall success rate by 8.3~percentage points (pp) -- reaching $-$19.3~pp on goal-conditioned tasks and $-$45~pp on individual tasks -- whereas sentence reordering, spatial-direction reversal, token noise, and even a 70B-parameter LLM crafting plausible-but-wrong plans all have negligible impact (within $\pm$4~pp). This asymmetry indicates that the action decoder depends on entity-reference integrity rather than reasoning quality or sequential structure. Notably, a sophisticated LLM-based attacker underperforms simple mechanical object-name substitution, because preserving plausibility inadvertently retains the entity-grounding structure the decoder needs. A cross-architecture control using a non-reasoning VLA confirms the vulnerability is exclusive to reasoning-augmented models, while instruction-level attacks degrade both architectures -- establishing that the internal reasoning trace is a distinct and stealthy threat vector invisible to input-validation defenses.

</details>


### [75] [Easy-IIL: Reducing Human Operational Burden in Interactive Imitation Learning via Assistant Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12769)
*Chengjie Zhang,Chao Tang,Wenlong Dong,Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Easy-IIL框架使用现成的基于模型的模仿学习方法作为辅助专家，替代人类进行大部分数据收集，显著减少人类操作负担，同时保持交互式模仿学习性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交互式模仿学习需要大量人类参与离线演示和在线交互，现有研究主要关注减少被动监控而非主动操作的工作量。基于模型的模仿方法在低数据量时表现与端到端方法相当但需要更少演示，但数据增加时通常被超越

Method: 提出Easy-IIL框架：1) 人类专家提供单个演示初始化辅助专家；2) 使用现成的基于模型模仿方法作为辅助专家替代人类进行大部分数据收集；3) 人类仅在任务接近失败的关键状态进行干预；4) 保持离线数据和在线数据质量以维持IIL性能

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，Easy-IIL显著减少人类操作负担，同时保持与主流IIL基线相当的性能。用户研究进一步证实Easy-IIL降低了人类专家的主观工作负荷

Conclusion: Easy-IIL通过利用基于模型的模仿方法作为辅助专家，有效减少了交互式模仿学习中人类专家的主动操作负担，同时保持了学习性能，为降低IIL中人类参与成本提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Interactive Imitation Learning (IIL) typically relies on extensive human involvement for both offline demonstration and online interaction. Prior work primarily focuses on reducing human effort in passive monitoring rather than active operation. Interestingly, structured model-based imitation approaches achieve comparable performance with significantly fewer demonstrations than end-to-end imitation learning policies in the low-data regime. However, these methods are typically surpassed by end-to-end policies as the data increases. Leveraging this insight, we propose Easy-IIL, a framework that utilizes off-the-shelf model-based imitation methods as an assistant expert to replace active human operation for the majority of data collection. The human expert only provides a single demonstration to initialize the assistant expert and intervenes in critical states where the task is approaching failure. Furthermore, Easy-IIL can maintain IIL performance by preserving both offline and online data quality. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Easy-IIL significantly reduces human operational burden while maintaining performance comparable to mainstream IIL baselines. User studies further confirm that Easy-IIL reduces subjective workload on the human expert. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/easy-iil

</details>


### [76] [Motion-Specific Battery Health Assessment for Quadrotors Using High-Fidelity Battery Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12791)
*Joonhee Kim,Sanghyun Park,Donghyeong Kim,Eunseon Choi,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个端到端框架，用于评估四旋翼无人机飞行运动对电池健康的影响，通过实时电流监测和电化学模型分析不同飞行模式如何加速电池退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前四旋翼无人机的能量感知规划通常将电池视为简单的能量储存器，忽略了飞行运动引起的动态电流负载会加速电池退化的问题。需要建立运动感知的电池健康评估框架来平衡飞行效率与电池寿命。

Method: 1. 设计宽范围电流传感模块，在真实飞行中捕获运动特定的电流曲线，保留瞬态特征；2. 使用参考性能测试和基于退化耦合电化学模型的元启发式方法校准高保真电池模型；3. 通过模拟测量的飞行负载，系统分析不同飞行运动如何转化为退化模式（锂库存损失、活性材料损失和内部副反应）。

Result: 研究表明，即使两种飞行曲线消耗相同的平均能量，它们的瞬态负载结构也会驱动不同的退化路径。这强调了需要运动感知的电池管理来平衡效率与电池退化。

Conclusion: 提出了一个端到端的运动感知电池健康评估框架，通过结合实时电流监测和电化学模型，揭示了飞行运动对电池退化的具体影响机制，为优化四旋翼无人机电池管理提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Quadrotor endurance is ultimately limited by battery behavior, yet most energy aware planning treats the battery as a simple energy reservoir and overlooks how flight motions induce dynamic current loads that accelerate battery degradation. This work presents an end to end framework for motion aware battery health assessment in quadrotors. We first design a wide range current sensing module to capture motion specific current profiles during real flights, preserving transient features. In parallel, a high fidelity battery model is calibrated using reference performance tests and a metaheuristic based on a degradation coupled electrochemical model.By simulating measured flight loads in the calibrated model, we systematically resolve how different flight motions translate into degradation modes loss of lithium inventory and loss of active material as well as internal side reactions. The results demonstrate that even when two flight profiles consume the same average energy, their transient load structures can drive different degradation pathways, emphasizing the need for motion-aware battery management that balances efficiency with battery degradation.

</details>


### [77] [FLUX: Accelerating Cross-Embodiment Generative Navigation Policies via Rectified Flow and Static-to-Dynamic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12806)
*Zeying Gong,Yangyi Zhong,Yiyi Ding,Tianshuai Hu,Guoyang Zhao,Lingdong Kong,Rong Li,Jiadi You,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLUX：首个基于流的统一导航策略，通过线性化概率流实现高效推理，在动态导航基准DynBench上实现SOTA性能，并展示零样本仿真到现实迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航需要从静态目标到达到动态社交穿越的广泛技能，但现有评估方法分散在不同协议中，缺乏统一框架。需要开发能够处理多种导航任务的统一策略。

Method: 提出FLUX，首个基于流的统一导航策略，通过线性化概率流将迭代去噪替换为直线轨迹。采用静态到动态课程学习：先建立几何先验，再通过强化学习在动态社交环境中微调。使用随机动作分布捕捉恢复行为。

Result: FLUX在六个基本导航任务上实现SOTA性能。推理效率比先前基于流的方法提高47%，比基于扩散的方法提高29%。在轮式、四足和人形平台上实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，无需微调。

Conclusion: FLUX通过流线性化和静态到动态课程学习，实现了高效统一的导航策略，在多种任务上表现优异，并具备强大的仿真到现实迁移能力，为自主导航系统提供了全面解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation requires a broad spectrum of skills, from static goal-reaching to dynamic social traversal, yet evaluation remains fragmented across disparate protocols. We introduce DynBench, a dynamic navigation benchmark featuring physically valid crowd simulation. Combined with existing static protocols, it supports comprehensive evaluation across six fundamental navigation tasks. Within this framework, we propose FLUX, the first flow-based unified navigation policy. By linearizing probability flow, FLUX replaces iterative denoising with straight-line trajectories, improving per-step inference efficiency by 47% over prior flow-based methods and 29% over diffusion-based ones. Following a static-to-dynamic curriculum, FLUX initially establishes geometric priors and is subsequently refined through reinforcement learning in dynamic social environments. This regime not only strengthens socially-aware navigation but also enhances static task robustness by capturing recovery behaviors through stochastic action distributions. FLUX achieves state-of-the-art performance across all tasks and demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on wheeled, quadrupedal, and humanoid platforms without any fine-tuning.

</details>


### [78] [SmoothTurn: Learning to Turn Smoothly for Agile Navigation with Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12842)
*Zunzhi You,Haolan Guo,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmoothTurn：一种用于四足机器人敏捷顺序局部导航的学习型控制框架，通过顺序目标到达奖励、前瞻观测空间和自动目标课程，实现高速奔跑时的平滑转向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目标导航方法在顺序目标切换时无法预见即将到来的机动动作或保持动量，限制了四足机器人在火灾救援、工业检查等紧急应用中的敏捷导航潜力

Method: 将任务重新表述为顺序局部导航，提出SmoothTurn框架：采用顺序目标到达奖励、包含未来目标前瞻窗口的扩展观测空间，以及基于目标到达性能逐步增加难度的自动目标课程

Result: 训练的策略可直接部署在配备机载传感器和计算设备的真实四足机器人上，仿真和真实世界实验均显示该策略能实现跨目标的平滑转向，并涌现出控制动量、提前面向未来目标、规划高效路径等行为

Conclusion: SmoothTurn成功解决了顺序目标导航中的平滑转向问题，使四足机器人能够充分发挥其敏捷潜力，为紧急应用中的快速导航提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots show great potential for valuable real-world applications such as fire rescue and industrial inspection. Such applications often require urgency and the ability to navigate agilely, which in turn demands the capability to change directions smoothly while running in high speed. Existing approaches for agile navigation typically learn a single-goal reaching policy by encouraging the robot to stay at the target position after reaching there. As a result, when the policy is used to reach sequential goals that require changing directions, it cannot anticipate upcoming maneuvers or maintain momentum across the switch of goals, thereby preventing the robot from fully exploiting its agility potential. In this work, we formulate the task as sequential local navigation, extending the single-goal-conditioned local navigation formulation in prior work. We then introduce SmoothTurn, a learning-based control framework that learns to turn smoothly while running rapidly for agile sequential local navigation. The framework adopts a novel sequential goal-reaching reward, an expanded observation space with a lookahead window for future goals, and an automatic goal curriculum that progressively expands the difficulty of sampled goal sequences based on the goal-reaching performance. The trained policy can be directly deployed on real quadrupedal robots with onboard sensors and computation. Both simulation and real-world empirical results show that SmoothTurn learns an agile locomotion policy that performs smooth turning across goals, with emergent behaviors such as controlling momentum when switching goals, facing towards the future goal in advance, and planning efficient paths. We have provided video demos of the learned motions in the supplementary materials. The source code and trained policies will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>
