### [79] [Consistent and Efficient MSCKF-based LiDAR-Inertial Odometry with Inferred Cluster-to-Plane Constraints for UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12904)
*Jinwen Zhu,Xudong Zhao,Fangcheng Zhu,Jun Hu,Shi Jin,Yinian Mao,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向无人机的高效紧耦合激光雷达-惯性里程计框架，通过空问投影消除特征参数依赖，采用并行体素数据关联和紧凑聚类到平面测量模型，在保持精度的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机导航对精度和鲁棒性要求高，但现有激光雷达-惯性里程计系统在尺寸、重量和功率受限平台上存在估计不一致性和计算瓶颈问题，需要专门针对无人机平台设计更高效、一致的解决方案。

Method: 在高效多状态约束卡尔曼滤波器框架下，构建滑动窗口内平面特征的共面约束，通过空问投影消除状态向量中特征参数的直接依赖；引入并行体素数据关联和紧凑聚类到平面测量模型，无损降低观测维度，加速更新过程。

Result: 方法在一致性和效率方面优于大多数先进方法，在退化场景中表现出更好的鲁棒性，通过无地图特性实现最低内存使用，可在资源受限的嵌入式平台（如NVIDIA Jetson TX2）上实时运行。

Conclusion: 提出的紧耦合激光雷达-惯性里程计框架为无人机平台提供了卓越的一致性和效率平衡，解决了现有系统在资源受限环境中的关键问题，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Robust and accurate navigation is critical for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) especially for those with stringent Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints. However, most state-of-the-art (SOTA) LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (LIO) systems still suffer from estimation inconsistency and computational bottlenecks when deployed on such platforms. To address these issues, this paper proposes a consistent and efficient tightly-coupled LIO framework tailored for UAVs. Within the efficient Multi-State Constraint Kalman Filter (MSCKF) framework, we build coplanar constraints inferred from planar features observed across a sliding window. By applying null-space projection to sliding-window coplanar constraints, we eliminate the direct dependency on feature parameters in the state vector, thereby mitigating overconfidence and improving consistency. More importantly, to further boost the efficiency, we introduce a parallel voxel-based data association and a novel compact cluster-to-plane measurement model. This compact measurement model losslessly reduces observation dimensionality and significantly accelerating the update process. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method outperforms most state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches by providing a superior balance of consistency and efficiency. It exhibits improved robustness in degenerate scenarios, achieves the lowest memory usage via its map-free nature, and runs in real-time on resource-constrained embedded platforms (e.g., NVIDIA Jetson TX2).

</details>


### [80] [GoalSwarm: Multi-UAV Semantic Coordination for Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12908)
*MoniJesu Wonders James,Amir Atef Habel,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserokou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoalSwarm：完全去中心化的多无人机框架，用于零样本语义目标导航，通过共享轻量级2D语义占据地图、零样本基础模型SAM3和贝叶斯价值地图实现开放词汇目标识别和协调探索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人团队在未知环境中进行协作视觉语义导航面临两大挑战：部署重型感知模型的机载计算限制，以及去中心化多智能体协调的复杂性。现有方法难以实现鲁棒的开放词汇目标导航。

Method: 1. 每个无人机从空中视角投影深度观测，协作构建共享的轻量级2D自上而下语义占据地图，避免完整3D表示的计算负担；2. 集成零样本基础模型SAM3进行开放词汇检测和像素级分割；3. 提出贝叶斯价值地图，融合多视角检测置信度生成像素级目标相关性分布；4. 采用结合语义前沿提取、成本-效用投标（使用测地线路径成本）和空间分离惩罚的去中心化协调策略。

Result: 论文提出了GoalSwarm框架，通过轻量级2D表示、零样本感知和去中心化协调，实现了多无人机在未知环境中的开放词汇语义目标导航，避免了重型模型的计算负担和集中式协调的复杂性。

Conclusion: GoalSwarm为多无人机团队提供了一种计算高效、完全去中心化的零样本语义目标导航解决方案，通过结合轻量级地图表示、开放词汇感知和智能协调策略，解决了现有方法在计算约束和协调复杂性方面的挑战。

Abstract: Cooperative visual semantic navigation is a foundational capability for aerial robot teams operating in unknown environments. However, achieving robust open-vocabulary object-goal navigation remains challenging due to the computational constraints of deploying heavy perception models onboard and the complexity of decentralized multi-agent coordination. We present GoalSwarm, a fully decentralized multi-UAV framework for zero-shot semantic object-goal navigation. Each UAV collaboratively constructs a shared, lightweight 2D top-down semantic occupancy map by projecting depth observations from aerial vantage points, eliminating the computational burden of full 3D representations while preserving essential geometric and semantic structure. The core contributions of GoalSwarm are threefold: (1) integration of zero-shot foundation model -- SAM3 for open vocabulary detection and pixel-level segmentation, enabling open-vocabulary target identification without task-specific training; (2) a Bayesian Value Map that fuses multi-viewpoint detection confidences into a per-pixel goal-relevance distribution, enabling informed frontier scoring via Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) exploration; and (3) a decentralized coordination strategy combining semantic frontier extraction, cost-utility bidding with geodesic path costs, and spatial separation penalties to minimize redundant exploration across the swarm.

</details>


### [81] [MotionAnymesh: Physics-Grounded Articulation for Simulation-Ready Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12936)
*WenBo Xu,Liu Liu,Li Zhang,Dan Guo,RuoNan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionAnymesh：一个零样本自动化框架，将静态3D网格转换为可交互的铰接式数字孪生体，通过物理先验消除运动学幻觉，并保证无碰撞铰接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本方法在处理复杂资产时缺乏物理基础，导致运动学幻觉和网格穿透问题，限制了在具身AI和机器人仿真中的应用。

Method: 1) 运动学感知部件分割模块：结合SP4D物理先验来约束视觉语言模型推理；2) 几何-物理联合估计管道：采用类型感知初始化和物理约束轨迹优化，确保无碰撞铰接。

Result: 在几何精度和动态物理可执行性方面显著优于现有基线，为下游应用提供高可靠性资产。

Conclusion: MotionAnymesh成功解决了静态网格到可仿真数字孪生的转换问题，通过物理基础方法消除了运动学幻觉并保证了无碰撞铰接，推动了具身AI和机器人仿真的发展。

Abstract: Converting static 3D meshes into interactable articulated assets is crucial for embodied AI and robotic simulation. However, existing zero-shot pipelines struggle with complex assets due to a critical lack of physical grounding. Specifically, ungrounded Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently suffer from kinematic hallucinations, while unconstrained joint estimation inevitably leads to catastrophic mesh inter-penetration during physical simulation. To bridge this gap, we propose MotionAnymesh, an automated zero-shot framework that seamlessly transforms unstructured static meshes into simulation-ready digital twins. Our method features a kinematic-aware part segmentation module that grounds VLM reasoning with explicit SP4D physical priors, effectively eradicating kinematic hallucinations. Furthermore, we introduce a geometry-physics joint estimation pipeline that combines robust type-aware initialization with physics-constrained trajectory optimization to rigorously guarantee collision-free articulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MotionAnymesh significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both geometric precision and dynamic physical executability, providing highly reliable assets for downstream applications.

</details>


### [82] [RoboStream: Weaving Spatio-Temporal Reasoning with Memory in Vision-Language Models for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12939)
*Yuzhi Huang,Jie Wu,Weijue Bu,Ziyi Xiong,Gaoyang Jiang,Ye Li,Kangye Ji,Shuzhao Xie,Yue Huang,Chenglei Wu,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboStream：无需训练的机器人操作框架，通过时空融合令牌实现几何锚定，通过因果时空图记录状态转换，显著提升长时程操作性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的规划器将每个步骤视为孤立的观测-动作映射，需要从原始像素中反复推断场景几何，且无法跟踪先前动作对环境的影响。这些系统缺乏时空推理能力，导致感知误差累积、被遮挡物体被遗忘、前提条件违反等问题，限制了长时程操作的可靠性。

Method: 提出RoboStream框架，包含两个核心组件：1）时空融合令牌（STF-Tokens），将视觉证据绑定到3D几何属性，实现持久对象锚定；2）因果时空图（CSTG），记录跨步骤的动作触发状态转换，保持因果连续性。该框架无需额外训练或微调。

Result: 在长时程RLBench任务上达到90.5%成功率，在具有挑战性的真实世界积木搭建任务上达到44.4%成功率。相比之下，SoFar和VoxPoser在真实世界任务上仅获得11.1%成功率，表明时空推理和因果记忆是长时程操作的关键缺失组件。

Conclusion: RoboStream通过引入持久几何锚定和因果状态跟踪，解决了VLM规划器在长时程操作中的关键限制。时空融合令牌和因果时空图的设计使系统能够追踪因果链并在遮挡下保持对象恒常性，显著提升了长时程操作的可靠性。

Abstract: Enabling reliable long-horizon robotic manipulation is a crucial step toward open-world embodied intelligence. However, VLM-based planners treat each step as an isolated observation-to-action mapping, forcing them to reinfer scene geometry from raw pixels at every decision point while remaining unaware of how prior actions have reshaped the environment. Despite strong short-horizon performance, these systems lack the spatio-temporal reasoning required for persistent geometric anchoring and memory of action-triggered state transitions. Without persistent state tracking, perceptual errors accumulate across the execution horizon, temporarily occluded objects are catastrophically forgotten, and these compounding failures lead to precondition violations that cascade through subsequent steps. In contrast, humans maintain a persistent mental model that continuously tracks spatial relations and action consequences across interactions rather than reconstructing them at each instant. Inspired by this human capacity for causal spatio-temporal reasoning with persistent memory, we propose RoboStream, a training-free framework that achieves geometric anchoring through Spatio-Temporal Fusion Tokens (STF-Tokens), which bind visual evidence to 3D geometric attributes for persistent object grounding, and maintains causal continuity via a Causal Spatio-Temporal Graph (CSTG) that records action-triggered state transitions across steps. This design enables the planner to trace causal chains and preserve object permanence under occlusion without additional training or fine-tuning. RoboStream achieves 90.5% on long-horizon RLBench and 44.4% on challenging real-world block-building tasks, where both SoFar and VoxPoser score 11.1%, demonstrating that spatio-temporal reasoning and causal memory are critical missing components for reliable long-horizon manipulation.

</details>


### [83] [Coordinated Manipulation of Hybrid Deformable-Rigid Objects in Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12940)
*Anees Peringal,Anup Teejo Mathew,Panagiotis liatsis,Federico Renda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于准静态优化的混合可变形线性物体(hDLO)操作规划器，使用应变基Cosserat杆模型，在约束环境中实现刚柔混合组件的精确操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作研究主要集中在纯可变形线性物体(DLOs)，但对于包含刚性和可变形组件的混合组件在约束环境中的操作仍具挑战性。需要开发能够同时处理刚性和可变形元素的操作规划方法。

Method: 采用准静态优化方法，使用应变基Cosserat杆模型扩展刚体公式到hDLO。利用可变形链节的柔顺性在约束环境中操作，通过可微分模型和解析梯度实现高效计算，包括逆运动静力学(IKS)求解和轨迹优化。

Result: 方法在IKS问题上比有限差分基线快33倍；实验验证显示平均变形误差约3cm(可变形链节长度的5%)；与基于采样的可行性规划器相比，证明了所提最优规划器的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于优化的规划器能够有效处理约束环境中混合可变形线性物体的操作问题，通过解析梯度实现高效计算，为复杂混合组件的机器人操作提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Coordinated robotic manipulation of deformable linear objects (DLOs), such as ropes and cables, has been widely studied; however, handling hybrid assemblies composed of both deformable and rigid elements in constrained environments remains challenging. This work presents a quasi-static optimization-based manipulation planner that employs a strain-based Cosserat rod model, extending rigid-body formulations to hybrid deformable linear objects (hDLO). The proposed planner exploits the compliance of deformable links to maneuver through constraints while achieving task-space objectives for the object that are unreachable with rigid tools. By leveraging a differentiable model with analytically derived gradients, the method achieves up to a 33x speedup over finite-difference baselines for inverse kinetostatic(IKS) problems. Furthermore, the subsequent trajectory optimization problem, warm-started using the IKS solution, is only practically realizable via analytical derivatives. The proposed algorithm is validated in simulation on various hDLO systems and experimentally on a three-link hDLO manipulated in a constrained environment using a dual-arm robotic system. Experimental results confirm the planner's accuracy, yielding an average deformation error of approximately 3 cm (5% of the deformable link length) between the desired and measured marker positions. Finally, the proposed optimal planner is compared against a sampling-based feasibility planner adapted to the strain-based formulation. The results demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of the proposed approach for robotic manipulation of hybrid assemblies in constrained environments.

</details>


### [84] [ReMem-VLA: Empowering Vision-Language-Action Model with Memory via Dual-Level Recurrent Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12942)
*Hang Li,Fengyi Shen,Dong Chen,Liudi Yang,Xudong Wang,Jinkui Shi,Zhenshan Bing,Ziyuan Liu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReMem-VLA是一种具有循环记忆机制的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过可学习的查询机制解决机器人控制中长期记忆问题，在多种记忆任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常基于马尔可夫假设，在处理需要历史上下文的任务时容易出错。现有的记忆方法要么从记忆库中检索（易受干扰项误导），要么扩展帧窗口（固定时间范围限制长期记忆保留）。

Method: 引入ReMem-VLA模型，配备两套可学习查询：帧级循环记忆查询（在连续帧间传播信息支持短期记忆）和块级循环记忆查询（在时间块间传递上下文支持长期记忆）。这些查询通过端到端训练聚合和维护相关上下文，无需额外训练或推理成本。此外，引入过去观测预测作为辅助训练目标以增强视觉记忆。

Result: ReMem-VLA在记忆中心的仿真和真实机器人实验中表现出强大的多维度记忆能力，包括空间、序列、情景、时间和视觉记忆。显著优于无记忆VLA基线（π0.5和OpenVLA-OFT），并在记忆依赖任务上大幅超越MemoryVLA。

Conclusion: ReMem-VLA通过循环记忆查询机制有效解决了VLA模型中的长期记忆问题，在多种记忆任务上表现出色，为机器人控制中的记忆增强提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models for closed-loop robot control are typically cast under the Markov assumption, making them prone to errors on tasks requiring historical context. To incorporate memory, existing VLAs either retrieve from a memory bank, which can be misled by distractors, or extend the frame window, whose fixed horizon still limits long-term retention. In this paper, we introduce ReMem-VLA, a Recurrent Memory VLA model equipped with two sets of learnable queries: frame-level recurrent memory queries for propagating information across consecutive frames to support short-term memory, and chunk-level recurrent memory queries for carrying context across temporal chunks for long-term memory. These queries are trained end-to-end to aggregate and maintain relevant context over time, implicitly guiding the model's decisions without additional training or inference cost. Furthermore, to enhance visual memory, we introduce Past Observation Prediction as an auxiliary training objective. Through extensive memory-centric simulation and real-world robot experiments, we demonstrate that ReMem-VLA exhibits strong memory capabilities across multiple dimensions, including spatial, sequential, episodic, temporal, and visual memory. ReMem-VLA significantly outperforms memory-free VLA baselines $π$0.5 and OpenVLA-OFT and surpasses MemoryVLA on memory-dependent tasks by a large margin.

</details>


### [85] [Language-Grounded Decoupled Action Representation for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12967)
*Wuding Weng,Tongshu Wu,Liucheng Chen,Siyu Xie,Zheng Wang,Xing Xu,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LaDA框架通过语言引导的解耦动作表示，使用翻译、旋转和夹爪控制三个可解释的动作基元作为中间层，连接视觉语言理解和低级动作控制，提升机器人操作的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级视觉语言理解与低级动作控制之间的异构性仍然是机器人操作的基本挑战。现有方法虽然在特定任务的动作对齐方面有所进展，但在处理新颖或语义相关任务时，往往难以生成鲁棒且准确的动作。

Method: 提出语言引导的解耦动作表示（LaDA）框架，利用自然语言作为连接感知和控制的语义桥梁。引入三个可解释的动作基元（平移、旋转、夹爪控制）作为细粒度中间层，为低级动作提供明确的语义结构。采用语义引导的软标签对比学习目标来对齐跨任务的相似动作基元，增强泛化能力和运动一致性。受课程学习启发，采用自适应加权策略动态平衡对比学习和模仿学习目标，实现稳定有效的训练。

Result: 在模拟基准测试（LIBERO和MimicGen）和真实世界演示中进行了广泛实验，验证了LaDA在未见或相关任务上实现了强大的性能表现和有效的泛化能力。

Conclusion: LaDA框架通过语言引导的解耦动作表示，成功解决了视觉语言理解与动作控制之间的异构性问题，显著提升了机器人操作任务的泛化性能，为新颖和语义相关任务提供了鲁棒且准确的动作生成能力。

Abstract: The heterogeneity between high-level vision-language understanding and low-level action control remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Although recent methods have advanced task-specific action alignment, they often struggle to generate robust and accurate actions for novel or semantically related tasks. To address this, we propose the Language-Grounded Decoupled Action Representation (LaDA) framework, which leverages natural language as a semantic bridge to connect perception and control. LaDA introduces a fine-grained intermediate layer of three interpretable action primitives--translation, rotation, and gripper control--providing explicit semantic structure for low-level actions. It further employs a semantic-guided soft-label contrastive learning objective to align similar action primitives across tasks, enhancing generalization and motion consistency. An adaptive weighting strategy, inspired by curriculum learning, dynamically balances contrastive and imitation objectives for stable and effective training. Extensive experiments on simulated benchmarks (LIBERO and MimicGen) and real-world demonstrations validate that LaDA achieves strong performance and generalizes effectively to unseen or related tasks.

</details>


### [86] [Route Fragmentation Based on Resource-centric Prioritisation for Efficient Multi-Robot Path Planning in Agricultural Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12994)
*James R. Heselden,Gautham P. Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了两种基于优先级的片段规划器变体，作为资源中心的多机器人路径规划算法，用于农业环境中长期导航和运营规划，通过路线分段实现部分路线推进，显著提高了任务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业环境存在大量空间密集的导航瓶颈，现有基于智能体中心的多机器人路径规划方法从智能体角度解决冲突，而非从竞争资源角度出发。此外，这种竞争密度限制了空间交错的能力，而许多规划器依赖空间交错来实现高吞吐量。

Method: 提出了两种基于优先级的片段规划器变体，作为资源中心的多机器人路径规划算法。这些方法利用路线分段实现部分路线推进，并限制基于二进制的等待的影响。在代表商业聚酯隧道环境的3.6公里拓扑地图上进行终身仿真评估，与5种基线算法在不同机器人舰队规模下进行对比。

Result: 片段规划器相比优先级规划和基于优先级的搜索算法实现了显著的吞吐量增益。在相同时间周期内，任务吞吐量达到最优任务吞吐量的95%。

Conclusion: 对于在走廊主导的农业环境中长期部署农业机器人，资源中心的多机器人路径规划方法是实现高效运营规划的必要条件。

Abstract: Agricultural environments present high proportions of spatially dense navigation bottlenecks for long-term navigation and operational planning of agricultural mobile robots. The existing agent-centric multi-robot path planning (MRPP) approaches resolve conflicts from the perspective of agents, rather than from the resources under contention. Further, the density of such contentions limits the capabilities of spatial interleaving, a concept that many planners rely on to achieve high throughput. In this work, two variants of the priority-based Fragment Planner (FP) are presented as resource-centric MRPP algorithms that leverage route fragmentation to enable partial route progression and limit the impact of binary-based waiting. These approaches are evaluated in lifelong simulation over a 3.6km topological map representing a commercial polytunnel environment. Their performances are contrasted against 5 baseline algorithms with varying robotic fleet sizes. The Fragment Planners achieved significant gains in throughput compared with Prioritised Planning (PP) and Priority-Based Search (PBS) algorithms. They further demonstrated a task throughput of 95% of the optimal task throughput over the same time period. This work shows that, for long-term deployment of agricultural robots in corridor-dominant agricultural environments, resource-centric MRPP approaches are a necessity for high-efficacy operational planning.

</details>


### [87] [SldprtNet: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for CAD Generation in Language-Driven 3D Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13098)
*Ruogu Li,Sikai Li,Yao Mu,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SldprtNet是一个包含24.2万工业零件的大规模数据集，支持语义驱动的CAD建模、几何深度学习和多模态3D设计模型训练，提供3D模型、参数化脚本、渲染图像和自然语言描述等多模态对齐数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD建模数据集缺乏大规模、多模态对齐的工业零件数据，难以支持语义驱动的CAD建模和跨模态学习。需要构建包含3D模型、参数化脚本、渲染图像和自然语言描述的综合数据集，以促进CAD生成任务的多模态方法研究。

Method: 1) 收集24.2万工业零件，提供.step和.sldprt格式3D模型；2) 开发编码器/解码器工具，支持13种CAD命令，实现3D模型与结构化文本表示的无损转换；3) 通过合并7个视角的渲染图像减少输入token长度；4) 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B多模态模型生成零件外观和功能的自然语言描述；5) 人工验证对齐描述、图像和3D模型；6) 在数据集子集上微调基线模型，比较图像+文本与纯文本输入的效果。

Result: 构建了包含24.2万样本的SldprtNet数据集，每个样本包含：3D模型文件、参数化建模脚本、7视角合成渲染图像、自然语言描述。实验结果表明，图像+文本的多模态输入在CAD生成任务中优于纯文本输入，验证了多模态数据集的价值。数据集具有真实工业零件、可扩展工具支持、多模态对齐和模型复杂度多样性等特点。

Conclusion: SldprtNet是一个为语义驱动CAD建模和跨模态学习构建的综合性多模态数据集，通过提供对齐的3D模型、参数化脚本、渲染图像和自然语言描述，支持CAD生成任务的多模态方法研究。实验结果证实了多模态数据集在CAD生成中的必要性和价值，为工业零件设计和几何深度学习提供了重要资源。

Abstract: We introduce SldprtNet, a large-scale dataset comprising over 242,000 industrial parts, designed for semantic-driven CAD modeling, geometric deep learning, and the training and fine-tuning of multimodal models for 3D design. The dataset provides 3D models in both .step and .sldprt formats to support diverse training and testing. To enable parametric modeling and facilitate dataset scalability, we developed supporting tools, an encoder and a decoder, which support 13 types of CAD commands and enable lossless transformation between 3D models and a structured text representation. Additionally, each sample is paired with a composite image created by merging seven rendered views from different viewpoints of the 3D model, effectively reducing input token length and accelerating inference. By combining this image with the parameterized text output from the encoder, we employ the lightweight multimodal language model Qwen2.5-VL-7B to generate a natural language description of each part's appearance and functionality. To ensure accuracy, we manually verified and aligned the generated descriptions, rendered images, and 3D models. These descriptions, along with the parameterized modeling scripts, rendered images, and 3D model files, are fully aligned to construct SldprtNet. To assess its effectiveness, we fine-tuned baseline models on a dataset subset, comparing image-plus-text inputs with text-only inputs. Results confirm the necessity and value of multimodal datasets for CAD generation. It features carefully selected real-world industrial parts, supporting tools for scalable dataset expansion, diverse modalities, and ensured diversity in model complexity and geometric features, making it a comprehensive multimodal dataset built for semantic-driven CAD modeling and cross-modal learning.

</details>


### [88] [Evaluating VLMs' Spatial Reasoning Over Robot Motion: A Step Towards Robot Planning with Motion Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13100)
*Wenxi Wu,Jingjing Zhang,Martim Brandão*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文评估了四种最先进的视觉语言模型在机器人运动空间推理方面的能力，使用四种查询方法，发现Qwen2.5-VL在零样本情况下达到71.4%准确率，微调后达到75%，而GPT-4o性能较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解用户指令和环境中物体空间关系对智能机器人系统至关重要。虽然视觉语言模型具有自然语言和空间推理能力，但尚不清楚它们在机器人运动规划中执行用户偏好或约束（如与物体的距离、拓扑属性或运动风格偏好）的空间推理能力程度。

Method: 评估四种最先进的视觉语言模型在机器人运动空间推理方面的能力，使用四种不同的查询方法。评估两种类型的运动偏好（物体接近度和路径风格），并分析准确性与计算成本（token数量）之间的权衡。

Result: 使用最高性能的查询方法时，Qwen2.5-VL在零样本情况下达到71.4%准确率，在较小模型微调后达到75%准确率，GPT-4o性能较低。研究还展示了准确性与计算成本之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 这项工作显示了视觉语言模型与机器人运动规划流程集成的潜力，尽管当前模型在空间推理方面仍有改进空间，但为未来研究提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: Understanding user instructions and object spatial relations in surrounding environments is crucial for intelligent robot systems to assist humans in various tasks. The natural language and spatial reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have the potential to enhance the generalization of robot planners on new tasks, objects, and motion specifications. While foundation models have been applied to task planning, it is still unclear the degree to which they have the capability of spatial reasoning required to enforce user preferences or constraints on motion, such as desired distances from objects, topological properties, or motion style preferences. In this paper, we evaluate the capability of four state-of-the-art VLMs at spatial reasoning over robot motion, using four different querying methods. Our results show that, with the highest-performing querying method, Qwen2.5-VL achieves 71.4% accuracy zero-shot and 75% on a smaller model after fine-tuning, and GPT-4o leads to lower performance. We evaluate two types of motion preferences (object-proximity and path-style), and we also analyze the trade-off between accuracy and computation cost in number of tokens. This work shows some promise in the potential of VLM integration with robot motion planning pipelines.

</details>


### [89] [A Feasibility-Enhanced Control Barrier Function Method for Multi-UAV Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13103)
*Qishen Zhong,Junlong Wu,Jian Yang,Guanwei Xiao,Junqi Wu,Zimeng Jiang,Pingan Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可行性增强控制屏障函数框架，解决多无人机密集场景中CBF-QP可行性问题，通过引入符号一致性约束提高内部兼容性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集多无人机场景中，多个CBF约束之间的内部不兼容性会损害CBF二次规划的可行性，影响碰撞避免性能

Method: 分析CBF约束的内部兼容性，推导内部兼容性的充分条件，引入符号一致性约束，结合最坏情况估计和松弛变量构建分散式CBF-QP框架

Result: 仿真结果显示该方法显著减少不可行性，在密集场景中相比现有基线提升碰撞避免性能，时变延迟下的仿真验证了鲁棒性，真实世界实验验证了实际应用性

Conclusion: 提出的FECBF框架有效解决了多无人机密集场景中CBF-QP的可行性问题，通过内部兼容性分析和符号一致性约束显著改善了碰撞避免性能

Abstract: This paper presents a feasibility-enhanced control barrier function (FECBF) framework for multi-UAV collision avoidance. In dense multi-UAV scenarios, the feasibility of the CBF quadratic program (CBF-QP) can be compromised due to internal incompatibility among multiple CBF constraints. To address this issue, we analyze the internal compatibility of CBF constraints and derive a sufficient condition for internal compatibility. Based on this condition, a sign-consistency constraint is introduced to mitigate internal incompatibility. The proposed constraint is incorporated into a decentralized CBF-QP formulation using worst-case estimates and slack variables. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces infeasibility and improves collision avoidance performance compared with existing baselines in dense scenarios. Additional simulations under varying time delays demonstrate the robustness of the proposed method. Real-world experiments validate the practical applicability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [90] [Panoramic Multimodal Semantic Occupancy Prediction for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13108)
*Guoqiang Zhao,Zhe Yang,Sheng Wu,Fei Teng,Mengfei Duan,Yuanfan Zheng,Kai Luo,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了首个用于四足机器人的全景多模态占据数据集PanoMMOcc和相应的感知框架VoxelHound，通过垂直抖动补偿和多模态信息提示融合模块，显著提升了在复杂环境下的三维占据预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占据预测方法主要针对轮式自动驾驶设计，严重依赖RGB线索，在复杂环境中鲁棒性不足。四足机器人的运动特性和全景成像需求需要专门的数据集和感知框架。

Method: 1) 创建PanoMMOcc数据集，包含四种传感模态的多样化场景数据；2) 提出VoxelHound框架，包含垂直抖动补偿模块缓解身体俯仰滚转引起的视角扰动，以及多模态信息提示融合模块联合利用全景视觉线索和辅助模态增强体素占据预测。

Result: 在PanoMMOcc基准测试中，VoxelHound实现了最先进的性能，mIoU提升4.16%。数据集和代码将公开提供以促进全景多模态三维感知研究。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了四足机器人全景多模态占据感知的空白，提出的数据集和框架为具身机器人系统在复杂环境中的三维感知提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Panoramic imagery provides holistic 360° visual coverage for perception in quadruped robots. However, existing occupancy prediction methods are mainly designed for wheeled autonomous driving and rely heavily on RGB cues, limiting their robustness in complex environments. To bridge this gap, (1) we present PanoMMOcc, the first real-world panoramic multimodal occupancy dataset for quadruped robots, featuring four sensing modalities across diverse scenes. (2) We propose a panoramic multimodal occupancy perception framework, VoxelHound, tailored for legged mobility and spherical imaging. Specifically, we design (i) a Vertical Jitter Compensation (VJC) module to mitigate severe viewpoint perturbations caused by body pitch and roll during mobility, enabling more consistent spatial reasoning, and (ii) an effective Multimodal Information Prompt Fusion (MIPF) module that jointly leverages panoramic visual cues and auxiliary modalities to enhance volumetric occupancy prediction. (3) We establish a benchmark based on PanoMMOcc and provide detailed data analysis to enable systematic evaluation of perception methods under challenging embodied scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that VoxelHound achieves state-of-the-art performance on PanoMMOcc (+4.16%} in mIoU). The dataset and code will be publicly released to facilitate future research on panoramic multimodal 3D perception for embodied robotic systems at https://github.com/SXDR/PanoMMOcc, along with the calibration tools released at https://github.com/losehu/CameraLiDAR-Calib.

</details>


### [91] [DecoVLN: Decoupling Observation, Reasoning, and Correction for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13133)
*Zihao Xin,Wentong Li,Yixuan Jiang,Bin Wang,Runming Cong,Jie Qin,Shengjun Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeCoVLN是一个用于长时程视觉语言导航的框架，通过自适应记忆库构建和状态-动作对级纠错微调来解决长期记忆构建和误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法面临两个主要挑战：构建有效的长期记忆库和克服误差累积问题。需要开发一个能够在复杂3D环境中进行稳健流式感知和闭环控制的框架。

Method: 1. 将长期记忆构建形式化为优化问题，引入自适应精炼机制，通过迭代优化统一评分函数从历史候选池中选择关键帧，平衡语义相关性、视觉多样性和时间覆盖度三个标准。2. 引入状态-动作对级纠错微调策略，利用状态间的测地距离精确量化与专家轨迹的偏差，在可信区域内收集高质量状态-动作对，过滤低相关性污染数据。

Result: 大量实验证明了DeCoVLN的有效性，并且已在真实世界环境中部署。

Conclusion: DeCoVLN通过自适应记忆库构建和纠错微调策略，有效解决了视觉语言导航中的长期记忆构建和误差累积问题，实现了稳健的长时程导航性能。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to follow long-horizon instructions and navigate complex 3D environments. However, existing approaches face two major challenges: constructing an effective long-term memory bank and overcoming the compounding errors problem. To address these issues, we propose DecoVLN, an effective framework designed for robust streaming perception and closed-loop control in long-horizon navigation. First, we formulate long-term memory construction as an optimization problem and introduce adaptive refinement mechanism that selects frames from a historical candidate pool by iteratively optimizing a unified scoring function. This function jointly balances three key criteria: semantic relevance to the instruction, visual diversity from the selected memory, and temporal coverage of the historical trajectory. Second, to alleviate compounding errors, we introduce a state-action pair-level corrective finetuning strategy. By leveraging geodesic distance between states to precisely quantify deviation from the expert trajectory, the agent collects high-quality state-action pairs in the trusted region while filtering out the polluted data with low relevance. This improves both the efficiency and stability of error correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of DecoVLN, and we have deployed it in real-world environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [DIALECTIC: A Multi-Agent System for Startup Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12274)
*Jae Yoon Bae,Simon Malberg,Joyce Galang,Andre Retterath,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DIALECTIC是一个基于LLM的多智能体系统，用于初创公司评估，通过模拟辩论生成投资决策分数，帮助VC投资者筛选机会


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险投资（VC）投资者面临大量投资机会但只能投资少数，早期筛选受限于投资者带宽，需要在评估严谨性和机会数量之间权衡

Method: 首先收集初创公司事实知识并组织成层次化问题树，然后合成支持和反对投资的自然语言论点，通过模拟辩论迭代批判和精炼论点，最终生成数值决策分数

Result: 在五个VC基金的真实投资机会上进行回测，显示DIALECTIC在预测初创公司成功方面与人类VC的精确度相当

Conclusion: DIALECTIC系统能够缓解VC投资者在早期筛选中的带宽限制问题，通过自动化评估帮助投资者更有效地优先排序投资机会

Abstract: Venture capital (VC) investors face a large number of investment opportunities but only invest in few of these, with even fewer ending up successful. Early-stage screening of opportunities is often limited by investor bandwidth, demanding tradeoffs between evaluation diligence and number of opportunities assessed. To ease this tradeoff, we introduce DIALECTIC, an LLM-based multi-agent system for startup evaluation. DIALECTIC first gathers factual knowledge about a startup and organizes these facts into a hierarchical question tree. It then synthesizes the facts into natural-language arguments for and against an investment and iteratively critiques and refines these arguments through a simulated debate, which surfaces only the most convincing arguments. Our system also produces numeric decision scores that allow investors to rank and thus efficiently prioritize opportunities. We evaluate DIALECTIC through backtesting on real investment opportunities aggregated from five VC funds, showing that DIALECTIC matches the precision of human VCs in predicting startup success.

</details>


### [93] [Collaborative Multi-Agent Optimization for Personalized Memory System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12631)
*Wenyu Mao,Haoyang Liu,Zhao Liu,Haosong Tan,Yaorui Shi,Jiancan Wu,An Zhang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CoMAM：一个协作强化学习框架，通过联合优化多智能体记忆系统中的局部智能体来促进协作，提升全局系统性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法专注于通过提示工程或微调独立优化局部智能体，但忽视了跨智能体协作，导致局部优化难以保证全局系统性能

Method: 提出协作强化学习框架CoMAM，将智能体执行建模为序列MDP以嵌入智能体间依赖，通过组级排名一致性量化各智能体贡献，作为自适应权重分配全局信用并整合局部-全局奖励

Result: 实验表明CoMAM优于领先的记忆系统，验证了协作强化学习联合优化的有效性

Conclusion: 通过协作强化学习联合优化多智能体记忆系统中的局部智能体，能够有效促进智能体协作，提升个性化LLM的全局性能

Abstract: Memory systems are crucial to personalized LLMs by mitigating the context window limitation in capturing long-term user-LLM conversations. Typically, such systems leverage multiple agents to handle multi-granular memory construction and personalized memory retrieval tasks. To optimize the system, existing methods focus on specializing agents on their local tasks independently via prompt engineering or fine-tuning. However, they overlook cross-agent collaboration, where independent optimization on local agents hardly guarantees the global system performance. To address this issue, we propose a Collaborative Reinforcement Learning Framework for Multi-Agent Memory Systems (CoMAM), jointly optimizing local agents to facilitate collaboration. Specifically, we regularize agents' execution as a sequential Markov decision process (MDP) to embed inter-agent dependencies into the state transition, yielding both local task rewards (e.g., information coverage for memory construction) and global rewards (i.e., query-answer accuracy). Then, we quantify each agent's contribution via group-level ranking consistency between local and global rewards, treating them as adaptive weights to assign global credit and integrate local-global rewards. Each agent is optimized by these integrated rewards, aligning local improvements with the global performance. Experiments show CoMAM outperforms leading memory systems, validating the efficacy of our proposed collaborative reinforcement learning for joint optimization.

</details>


### [94] [A Generative Model of Conspicuous Consumption and Status Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13220)
*Logan Cross,Jordi Grau-Moya,William A. Cunningham,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets,Joel Z. Leibo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于适当性理论的计算状态理论，通过LLM智能体模拟验证社会观察和预测模式完成的反馈循环如何内在地产生地位符号，并展示了功能需求如何转变为地位寻求行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理论如成本信号理论将偏好视为固定，难以解释符号意义如何随上下文变化或动态漂移，偶尔达到临界点。需要解释特定商品、信号或行为如何获得声望的生成机制。

Method: 在Concordia框架中使用基于大型语言模型的智能体进行模拟，通过实验操纵自然主义智能体日常生活中的社会可见性，观察社会互动如何将功能需求转化为地位寻求行为。

Result: 观察到真实世界奢侈品和程序生成合成商品的价格上涨和正价格弹性（凡勃伦效应），排除了预训练偏差作为唯一驱动因素。发现"影响者"智能体通过有针对性的制裁能够驱动不同亚文化的内源性形成，且类似的社会影响效应推广到非货币信号行为。

Conclusion: 这项工作在微观认知和宏观经济社会现象之间建立了生成性桥梁，为预测文化惯例如何从互动中涌现提供了新方法，展示了地位符号如何通过社会观察和预测模式完成的反馈循环内在地产生。

Abstract: Status signaling drives human behavior and the allocation of scarce resources such as mating opportunities, yet the generative mechanisms governing how specific goods, signals, or behaviors acquire prestige remain a puzzle. Classical frameworks, such as Costly Signaling Theory, treat preferences as fixed and struggle to explain how semiotic meaning changes based on context or drifts dynamically over time, occasionally reaching tipping points. In this work, we propose a computational theory of status grounded in the theory of appropriateness, positing that status symbols emerge endogenously through a feedback loop of social observation and predictive pattern completion. We validate this theory using simulations of groups of Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents in the Concordia framework. By experimentally manipulating social visibility within naturalistic agent daily routines, we demonstrate that social interactions transform functional demand into status-seeking behavior. We observe the emergence of price run-ups and positive price elasticity (Veblen effects) for both real-world luxury items and procedurally generated synthetic goods, ruling out pretraining bias as the sole driver. Furthermore, we demonstrate that "influencer" agents can drive the endogenous formation of distinct subcultures through targeted sanctioning, and find that similar social influence effects generalize to non-monetary signaling behaviors. This work provides a generative bridge between micro-level cognition and macro-level economic and sociological phenomena, offering a new methodology for forecasting how cultural conventions emerge from interaction.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [A Physics-Based Digital Human Twin for Galvanic-Coupling Wearable Communication Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12899)
*Silvia Mura,Chiara Cavigliano,Anna Marcucci,Pietro Savazzi,Anna Vizziello,Maurizio Magarini*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文系统表征了可穿戴式电耦合（GC）信道在窄带和宽带操作下的特性，通过物理一致的数字人体孪生模型将解剖特性、传播几何和电极-皮肤界面映射为可直接用于通信分析的复传递函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可穿戴式电耦合信道在不同带宽下的特性，建立组织电磁特性与波形失真之间的定量联系，为可穿戴GC系统的带宽、接口设计和收发器复杂度之间的权衡提供依据。

Method: 使用物理一致的数字人体孪生模型，将解剖特性、传播几何和电极-皮肤界面映射为复传递函数，评估纵向和径向配置下的衰减、相位延迟和群延迟，并通过衰减纹波和延迟标准差指标量化色散引起的变异性。

Result: 在10 kHz-1 MHz范围内确认了电准静态、弱色散行为；衰减主要由几何结构驱动，而幅度纹波和延迟变异性随带宽增加而增加；界面调节（凝胶和泡沫）显著改善了幅度和相位稳定性；传播几何决定了链路预算和基线延迟。

Conclusion: 该框架定量地将组织电磁特性与波形失真联系起来，使可穿戴GC系统能够在带宽、接口设计和收发器复杂度之间做出明智的权衡决策。

Abstract: This paper presents a systematic characterization of wearable galvanic coupling (GC) channels under narrowband and wideband operation. A physics-consistent digital human twin maps anatomical properties, propagation geometry, and electrode-skin interfaces into complex transfer functions directly usable for communication analysis. Attenuation, phase delay, and group delay are evaluated for longitudinal and radial configurations, and dispersion-induced variability is quantified through attenuation ripple and delay standard deviation metrics versus bandwidth. Results confirm electro-quasistatic, weakly dispersive behavior over 10 kHz-1 MHz. Attenuation is primarily geometry-driven, whereas amplitude ripple and delay variability increase with bandwidth, tightening equalization and synchronization constraints. Interface conditioning (gel and foam) significantly improves amplitude and phase stability, while propagation geometry governs link budget and baseline delay. Overall, the framework quantitatively links tissue electromagnetics to waveform distortion, enabling informed trade-offs among bandwidth, interface design, and transceiver complexity in wearable GC systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [LLMs for Human Mobility: Opportunities, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12420)
*Jie Gao,Yaoxin Wu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文对基于大语言模型的人类移动性研究进行了系统性综述，涵盖五个核心任务，分析了LLM在该领域的作用、挑战和设计模式，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动性研究关注人们在有意义地点间的移动模式及其对可达性、拥堵、排放和公共健康的影响。传统方法难以捕捉地点语义、出行者意图偏好和现实约束，而大语言模型能够处理这些语义信息，但相关研究分散且缺乏系统性综述。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，将LLM在人类移动性领域的应用归纳为五个核心任务：旅行行程规划、轨迹生成、移动性模拟、移动性预测、移动性语义与理解。针对每个任务，分析代表性工作、核心挑战与LLM的具体作用，总结典型的LLM解决方案设计模式。

Result: 建立了LLM在人类移动性研究中的任务-挑战-设计框架，系统梳理了各任务中LLM的应用模式，包括语义理解、约束建模、意图推理等。识别了当前研究在可靠性、基础性和隐私保护方面的局限性。

Conclusion: LLM为人类移动性研究提供了强大的语义处理能力，但需要解决可靠性、基础性和隐私保护等挑战。未来应发展更可靠、基础扎实且隐私保护的LLM方法，推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Human mobility studies how people move among meaningful places over time and how these movements aggregate into population-level patterns that shape accessibility, congestion, emissions, and public health. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in this domain because many human mobility problems require reasoning about place and activity semantics, travelers' intentions and preferences, and diverse real-world constraints that are difficult to capture using coordinates and other purely numerical attributes. Despite rapid growth, the literature is still scattered, and there is no clear overview that connects human mobility tasks, challenges, and LLM designs in a consistent way. This survey therefore provides a comprehensive synthesis of LLM-based research on human mobility across five tasks, including travel itinerary planning, trajectory generation, mobility simulation, mobility prediction, and mobility semantics and understanding. For each task, we review representative work, connect core challenges to the specific roles of LLMs, and summarize typical LLM-based solution designs. We conclude with open challenges and research directions toward reliable, grounded and privacy-aware LLM-based approaches for human mobility.

</details>


### [97] [ELLA: Generative AI-Powered Social Robots for Early Language Development at Home](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12508)
*Victor Nikhil Antony,Shiye Cao,Shuning Wang,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ELLA是一个基于生成式AI的社交机器人，通过互动讲故事、家长选择语言目标和支架式对话支持幼儿早期语言发展，经过家庭设计工作坊和8天部署测试验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期语言发展对儿童后续读写能力和学习至关重要，但许多家庭难以获得可扩展的高质量家庭支持。虽然生成式AI使社交机器人能够超越脚本化互动，但如何为学龄前儿童设计此类系统以及儿童在家庭环境中如何长期参与仍不明确。

Method: 采用多阶段人本设计过程：1) 访谈7名家长和5名教育工作者；2) 通过12次家庭设计工作坊迭代改进ELLA；3) 在10名儿童家中部署ELLA进行8天测试。系统包含互动讲故事、家长选择语言目标和支架式对话功能。

Result: 报告了家庭设计工作坊的设计洞察，描述了部署期间儿童的参与和行为特征，并提炼了生成式AI社交机器人支持家庭早期语言学习的设计启示。

Conclusion: ELLA展示了生成式AI社交机器人在家庭环境中支持幼儿早期语言发展的可行性，通过人本设计过程获得了关键的设计洞察，为未来类似系统的开发提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Early language development shapes children's later literacy and learning, yet many families have limited access to scalable, high-quality support at home. Recent advances in generative AI make it possible for social robots to move beyond scripted interactions and engage children in adaptive, conversational activities, but it remains unclear how to design such systems for pre-schoolers and how children engage with them over time in the home. We present ELLA (Early Language Learning Agent), an autonomous, generative AI-powered social robot that supports early language development through interactive storytelling, parent-selected language targets, and scaffolded dialogue. Using a multi-phased, human-centered process, we interviewed parents (n=7) and educators (n=5) and iteratively refined ELLA through twelve in-home design workshops. We then deployed ELLA with ten children for eight days. We report design insights from in-home workshops, characterize children's engagement and behaviors during deployment, and distill design implications for generative AI-powered social robots supporting early language learning at home.

</details>


### [98] [Exploring the Role of User Comments Throughout the Stages of Video-Based Task-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12509)
*Nayoung Kim,Yotam Sechayk,Zhongyi Zhou,Takeo Igarashi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨视频学习任务中评论区的角色，发现评论对学习至关重要但整合不足，提出设计改进机会


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频学习虽然动态直观，但可能遗漏隐性知识和细节，用户独特情境需要个性化适应，评论区成为重要补充资源，但缺乏对用户如何利用评论进行任务学习的深入理解

Method: 通过14名用户的访谈和8名用户的共同观看会话，探索视频任务学习中评论的作用

Result: 研究发现评论对学习至关重要，但在学习过程的所有阶段整合效果不佳，用户难以有效利用评论资源

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了改进视频任务学习中评论利用的设计机会，以更好地整合评论资源到学习过程中

Abstract: Learning tasks through videos is a dynamic way to acquire skills by witnessing entire processes. However, compared to in-person demonstrations, videos may omit tacit knowledge, including subtle details and contextual nuances. Users' unique circumstances, like missing ingredients in a recipe, may also require adaptation beyond the video content. To fill these gaps, many users turn to the comment section, seeking additional guidance and interactions with creators or peers to personalize their experience. Despite their importance, there is limited understanding of how users engage with and apply comments in task-learning scenarios. In our study, we explore the role of comments in video-based task-learning through interviews with 14 users, and co-watching sessions with eight. Our findings show that while comments are critical for learning, they are poorly integrated into all stages of the learning process. Based on our findings, we outline design opportunities to better utilize comments in video-based task-learning.

</details>


### [99] [Applying Value Sensitive Design to Location-Based Services: Designing for Shared Spaces and Local Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12521)
*Hiruni Kegalle,Flora D. Salim,Mark Sanderson,Jeffrey Chan,Danula Hettiachchi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出位置感知价值敏感设计（LA-VSD），针对位置服务的空间共享特性，通过三个启发式方法帮助设计者识别利益相关者、捕捉情境价值冲突、支持跨数字物理层的价值对齐交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置服务（如网约车、住宿、外卖等）将数字系统与物理空间交织，其影响超越用户范围，扩展到共享同一环境的其他人。现有设计方法难以应对共享物理空间中的价值冲突和位置特定情境的双重挑战。

Method: 提出位置感知价值敏感设计（LA-VSD），这是针对LBS独特特性的领域特定VSD适配。包含三个启发式方法：1）通过本地空间共享场景识别和优先考虑利益相关者；2）调整实证方法以捕捉情境中的价值和冲突；3）支持跨数字和物理服务层的价值对齐交互。

Result: 通过澳大利亚墨尔本的电动滑板车共享案例研究，展示了LA-VSD如何实现更接地气、情境感知和可操作的LBS设计。

Conclusion: LA-VSD为位置服务设计提供了更系统化的方法，能够更好地处理共享物理空间中的价值冲突和本地特定情境，使设计更加扎根实际、情境感知和可操作。

Abstract: Location-Based Services (LBS) such as ride-sharing, accommodation, food delivery, and location-driven social media platforms entangle digital systems with physical spaces, thereby generating impacts that extend beyond users to others who share the same environments. Existing design approaches struggle to address the dual challenge of value tensions that arise in shared physical spaces and the locality-specific contexts in which LBS operate. To respond, we introduce Location-Aware Value Sensitive Design (LA-VSD), a domain-specific adaptation of VSD tailored to the distinctive characteristics of LBS. LA-VSD guides designers through three heuristics to help (1) identify and prioritise stakeholders through local space-sharing scenarios, (2) adapt empirical methods to capture values and tensions in context, and (3) support value-aligned interactions across both digital and physical layers of the service. Through a case study of e-scooter sharing in Melbourne, Australia, we demonstrate how LA-VSD enables more grounded, context-aware, and actionable design of LBS.

</details>


### [100] [Leveraging Head Movement for Navigating Off-Screen Content on Large Curved Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12620)
*A K M Amanat Ullah,David Ahlström,Khalad Hasan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探索了在大曲面显示器上使用头部运动控制360度工作空间导航的技术，通过比较不同速率控制函数和区域控制函数，发现多项式速率控制性能最佳，优于行业标准的控制器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大曲面显示器虽然适合显示360度内容（如3D地图），但通常只能显示180度视口，导致信息被遮挡在屏幕外。用户在与屏幕内容交互前会自然地转动头部朝向感兴趣区域，因此头部运动为工作空间操作提供了有前景的替代方案，可以将屏幕外内容带入视野。

Method: 研究探索了速率控制函数（线性、S型、多项式）和区域控制函数（连续、摩擦、中断、加法）来将头部旋转转换为工作空间控制。使用地图导航任务进行实验，比较头部控制技术与行业标准的控制器方法（点击拖动和摇杆推动）。

Result: 多项式速率控制表现最佳，实现了最快的试验时间和最高的主观评分。在第二个研究中，多项式头部控制技术在360度工作空间导航任务中表现优于行业标准的控制器方法（点击拖动和摇杆推动）。

Conclusion: 基于研究结果，提供了指导原则来指导未来为大曲面显示器设计360度工作空间导航技术。头部运动控制为曲面显示器上的360度内容导航提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large curved displays are ideal for viewing 360 degree content, such as 3D maps, but typically restrict users to a 180 degree viewport, leaving information off-screen. Since users naturally direct their heads toward regions on-screen before interacting, head movements offer a promising alternative for workspace manipulation to bring off-screen content into view. We explore rate control functions (linear, sigmoid, polynomial) and zone control functions (continuous, friction, interrupted, additive) to translate head rotations into workspace control, enabling users to access off-screen content. Polynomial rate control emerges as the best choice, achieving the fastest trial times and highest subjective ratings. Using a map navigation task, our second study demonstrates that users perform better with the polynomial head-based technique than with the industry-standard controller-based methods, click-and-drag and joystick-push, for 360\degree workspace navigation. Based on these findings, we provide guidelines to inform the design of future 360\degree workspace navigation techniques for large curved displays.

</details>


### [101] ["I Should Know, But I Dare Not Ask": From Understanding Challenges in Patient Journeys to Deriving Design Implications for North Korean Defectors' Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12632)
*Hyungwoo Song,Jeongha Kim,Minju Kim,Duhyung Kwak,Minjeong Shin,Bongwon suh,Hyunggu Jung*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文研究了朝鲜脱北者在韩国医疗系统中的就医障碍，开发了Medibridge移动原型系统，通过AI医生预演生成"辅助笔记"来改善临床沟通


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已知朝鲜脱北者在韩国医疗系统中面临困难，但他们在患者就医过程中的具体挑战尚未得到充分探索。研究旨在深入了解脱北者在就医旅程中的具体障碍，特别是临床咨询环节的沟通问题。

Method: 研究采用定性访谈方法，首先对10名朝鲜脱北者进行了关于8步患者旅程的访谈，识别出临床咨询是关键障碍。然后开发了Medibridge移动原型系统，允许用户在真实医院就诊前与AI医生进行预演，生成"辅助笔记"。最后对15名脱北者进行了系统评估。

Result: 研究发现临床咨询是脱北者的关键障碍，具体表现为三个挑战：症状表达困难、社会文化顾虑、语言差异。Medibridge系统的评估显示，使用者在感知沟通能力方面有显著改善，包括表达更清晰、社会文化顾虑减少、语言自信心增强。

Conclusion: 研究贡献包括：对朝鲜脱北者医疗挑战的实证理解、创新的AI驱动预演系统（帮助用户准备真实临床沟通）、以及为流离失所人群设计包容性技术的设计启示。Medibridge系统通过预演和生成辅助笔记有效改善了脱北者在医疗系统中的沟通障碍。

Abstract: While it is known that North Korean defectors (NKDs) struggle with South Korea's healthcare system, the specific challenges of their patient journey remain underexplored. To investigate this, we conducted interviews with 10 NKDs about an 8-step patient journey and identified the clinical consultation step as a critical barrier for all participants, marked by three key challenges: expressing symptoms, managing social and cultural concerns, and overcoming language differences. In response, we developed Medibridge, a mobile prototype that allows users to rehearse with an AI doctor before a real hospital visit to generate a tangible ``Helper Note'' for their actual consultation. Our evaluation with 15 NKDs showed improvements in perceived communication capability, including greater expression clarity, reduced social and cultural concerns, and enhanced linguistic confidence. Our contributions include an empirical understanding of NKDs' healthcare challenges, a novel AI-powered rehearsal system that prepares users for real-world clinical communication, and design implications for inclusive technologies for displaced populations.

</details>


### [102] [Seeing Eye to Eye: Enabling Cognitive Alignment Through Shared First-Person Perspective in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12701)
*Zhuyu Teng,Pei Chen,Yichen Cai,Ruoqing Lu,Zhaoqu Jiang,Jiayang Li,Weitao You,Lingyun Sun*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Eye2Eye是一个利用第一人称视角实现人机认知对齐的AR框架，通过联合注意力协调、可修正记忆和反思反馈三大组件，显著提升协作效率和信任度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉的AI助手在协作任务中存在两个关键鸿沟：沟通鸿沟（用户需将丰富的并行意图转化为语言命令）和理解鸿沟（AI难以解读微妙的具身线索），导致协作效率低下。

Method: 提出Eye2Eye框架，包含三个核心组件：1）联合注意力协调实现流畅的焦点对齐；2）可修正记忆维持演化的共同基础；3）反思反馈允许用户澄清和优化AI理解。在AR原型中实现该框架，并通过用户研究和后处理流程评估进行验证。

Result: 评估结果显示，Eye2Eye显著减少了任务完成时间和交互负荷，同时增加了用户信任度，证明其组件协同工作有效改善了人机协作。

Conclusion: Eye2Eye通过第一人称视角作为人机认知对齐的通道，成功解决了沟通和理解鸿沟，其三大组件的协同作用为构建更自然、高效的人机协作系统提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Despite advances in multimodal AI, current vision-based assistants often remain inefficient in collaborative tasks. We identify two key gulfs: a communication gulf, where users must translate rich parallel intentions into verbal commands due to the channel mismatch , and an understanding gulf, where AI struggles to interpret subtle embodied cues. To address these, we propose Eye2Eye, a framework that leverages first-person perspective as a channel for human-AI cognitive alignment. It integrates three components: (1) joint attention coordination for fluid focus alignment, (2) revisable memory to maintain evolving common ground, and (3) reflective feedback allowing users to clarify and refine AI's understanding. We implement this framework in an AR prototype and evaluate it through a user study and a post-hoc pipeline evaluation. Results show that Eye2Eye significantly reduces task completion time and interaction load while increasing trust, demonstrating its components work in concert to improve collaboration.

</details>


### [103] [Virtual reality for large-scale laboratories based on colorized point clouds: design and pedagogical impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12727)
*Lei Fan,Yuxin Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于WebVR的虚拟工程实验室，使用大规模彩色点云数据构建，通过Unity和Potree集成实现高保真可视化，支持沉浸式探索、交互式热点和应急疏散模拟，评估显示用户反馈积极。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统现场工程实验室培训受时间、可访问性和安全性限制，需要一种能够克服这些约束的替代方案来增强工程教育中的实验培训效果。

Method: 提出新颖的WebVR框架，集成Unity和Potree实现浏览器中高保真点云可视化，支持沉浸式第一人称探索、引导导航、交互热点（设备与安全信息）以及应急疏散模拟。

Result: 通过匿名问卷调查评估，所有参与者对系统在所有评估维度上均评为"良好"或"优秀"，特别强调沉浸式探索和自主学习的好处，定性反馈为虚拟环境未来改进提供依据。

Conclusion: 基于WebVR的虚拟实验室能有效补充传统现场实验室教学，提供可扩展、易访问、低风险平台，显著增强工程教育学习体验。

Abstract: Effective laboratory training is essential in engineering education, yet conventional on-site instruction is often constrained by time, accessibility, and safety considerations. To address these challenges, this study presents the design, implementation, and evaluation of a web-based virtual reality (WebVR) representation of a large-scale engineering laboratory constructed from massive colorized point cloud data. This study proposes a novel WebVR framework that integrates Unity and Potree for high-fidelity point-cloud visualization combined with advanced interactive capabilities in a browser-based virtual laboratory. It supports immersive first-person exploration, guided navigation, interactive hotspots conveying equipment and safety information, as well as emergency evacuation simulations. The usability, educational effectiveness, and overall acceptance of the virtual laboratory were evaluated through an anonymous questionnaire administered to students and laboratory staff. The results indicate overwhelmingly positive feedback, with all participants rating the system as "good" or "excellent" across all evaluation dimensions. Participants particularly emphasized the benefits of immersive exploration and self-directed learning. In addition, qualitative feedback was systematically analyzed to inform future enhancements of the virtual environment. Overall, the findings demonstrate that the WebVR-based virtual laboratory can effectively complement conventional on-site laboratory instruction, offering a scalable, accessible, and low-risk platform that enhances learning experiences in engineering education.

</details>


### [104] [Exploring the role of embodiment on intimacy perception in a multiparty collaborative task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12783)
*Amine Benamara,Céline Clavel,Brian Ravenet,Nicolas Sabouret,Julien Saunier*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究协作棋盘游戏中群体凝聚力的影响因素，特别是社会技能的作用，重点关注具身化对不同互动方面的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体凝聚力是协作棋盘游戏中定义良好运作群体的关键方面，涵盖从任务成功到人际关系发展的多个维度。研究旨在探究影响群体凝聚力的因素，特别是提升多实体间协作的相关社会技能

Method: 基于收集的人类协作棋盘游戏语料库，提出实验协议研究不同智能体具身化如何影响对这些智能体及整个群体的感知

Result: 未提供具体实验结果，但提出了协议设计和多智能体系统相关挑战的问题框架

Conclusion: 提出了研究具身化对群体凝聚力影响的问题框架，并概述了协议设计和多智能体系统面临的挑战

Abstract: During collaborative board games, cohesion represents a key aspect to define a well functionning group. From the success of the task to the developement of interpersonal relationship, this concept covers many aspects of group dynamics. The goal of our work is to investigate the factors that impact cohesion in a group, and specifically the relevant social skills that improve collaboration between multiple entities. In this article, we focus on the role of embodiement on different aspects of an interaction. We propose an experimental protocol, based on a collected corpus of humans playing a collaborative board game, to study how different agents' embodiment affect the perception of these agents and of the group as a whole. We conclude by presenting an outline of the problematics of the conception of the protocol and of multi-agent system related challenges.

</details>


### [105] [Interrogating Design Homogenization in Web Vibe Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13036)
*Donghoon Shin,Alice Gao,Rock Yuren Pang,Jaewook Lee,Katharina Reinecke,Emily Tseng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了生成式AI在网页设计"氛围编码"中可能导致的设计同质化风险，分析了其生命周期和社会技术危害，并提出以"生产性摩擦"为中心的缓解框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着非专业创作者越来越多地使用LLM进行"氛围编码"（通过描述美学和功能目标而非编写代码来创建网站），生成式AI倾向于同质化的特性可能在复杂的网页设计任务中导致设计多样性减少，限制互联网上的创造性表达。

Method: 1. 首先描述"氛围编码"的生命周期，识别同质化风险可能出现的阶段；2. 进行社会技术风险分析，解构网页氛围编码的潜在危害及其与设计同质化的相互作用；3. 提出以"生产性摩擦"为中心的缓解框架；4. 通过微观、中观和宏观层面的案例研究展示该框架如何帮助创作者挑战默认输出并保持设计多样性。

Result: 研究发现，追求无摩擦生成会加剧同质化及其危害。通过案例研究表明，以生产性摩擦为中心的框架能够赋予创作者挑战默认输出的能力，在AI中介的网页设计中保持多样化的表达。

Conclusion: 生成式AI在网页设计氛围编码中存在设计同质化风险，但通过引入生产性摩擦而非追求完全无摩擦的生成过程，可以缓解这些风险，帮助创作者保持设计多样性并挑战AI的默认输出模式。

Abstract: Generative AI is known for its tendency to homogenize, often reproducing dominant style conventions found in training data. However, it remains unclear how these homogenizing effects extend to complex structural tasks like web design. As lay creators increasingly turn to LLMs to 'vibe-code' websites -- prompting for aesthetic and functional goals rather than writing code -- they may inadvertently narrow the diversity of their designs, and limit creative expression throughout the internet. In this paper, we interrogate the possibility of design homogenization in web vibe coding. We first characterize the vibe coding lifecycle, pinpointing stages where homogenization risks may arise. We then conduct a sociotechnical risk analysis unpacking the potential harms of web vibe coding and their interaction with design homogenization. We identify that the push for frictionless generation can exacerbate homogenization and its harms. Finally, we propose a mitigation framework centered on the idea of productive friction. Through case studies at the micro, meso, and macro levels, we show how centering productive friction can empower creators to challenge default outputs and preserve diverse expression in AI-mediated web design.

</details>


### [106] [Navig-AI-tion: Navigation by Contextual AI and Spatial Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13200)
*Mathias N. Lystbæk,Haley Adams,Ranjith Kagathi Ananda,Eric J Gonzalez,Luca Ballan,Qiuxuan Wu,Andrea Colaço,Peter Tan,Mar Gonzalez-Franco*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出结合视觉语言模型与空间音频提示的导航系统，通过环境地标锚定导航指令，并在用户方向错误时提供方向性空间音频信号，显著减少路线偏差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纯音频导航系统依赖模糊的方位指示，缺乏实时环境上下文，导致用户容易迷失方向并频繁出错，需要更有效的方向引导机制

Method: 集成视觉语言模型与空间音频提示：VLM提取环境地标来锚定导航指令，当用户面向错误方向时提供指示精确转向方向的空间音频信号

Result: 用户研究（n=12）显示：带空间音频提示的VLM系统相比纯VLM和Google Maps（纯音频）基准系统减少了路线偏差；用户反馈空间音频提示有效支持方向定位，地标锚定指令提供比纯音频Google Maps更好的导航体验

Conclusion: 该研究初步探索了未来纯音频导航系统整合方向性提示的实用性，特别是实时纠正性空间音频的应用潜力

Abstract: Audio-only walking navigation can leave users disoriented, relying on vague cardinal directions and lacking real-time environmental context, leading to frequent errors. To address this, we present a novel system that integrates a Vision Language Model (VLM) with a spatial audio cue. Our system extracts environmental landmarks to anchor navigation instructions and, crucially, provides a directional spatial audio signal when the user faces the wrong direction, indicating the precise turn direction. In a user study (n=12), the spatial audio cue with VLM reduced route deviations compared to both VLM-only and Google Maps (audio-only) baseline systems. Users reported that the spatial audio cue effectively supported orientation and that landmark-anchored instructions provided a better navigation experience over audio-only Google Maps. This work serves as an initial look at the utility of future audio-only navigation systems for incorporating directional cues, especially real-time corrective spatial audio.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Seeing the Trees for the Forest: Leveraging Tree-Shaped Substructures in Property Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12476)
*Daniel Aarao Reis Arturi,Christoph Köhnen,George Fletcher,Bettina Kemme,Stefanie Scherzinger*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文提出将树形子结构作为图数据库中的一等公民进行系统管理，通过实验证明利用XML数据库的结构索引可以显著加速路径查询，并提出了从建模到查询处理的完整生命周期管理愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图数据库系统对属性图中普遍存在的树形子结构缺乏原生支持：图模式无法捕获这些结构，查询语言和查询引擎也缺乏系统管理能力。这导致在处理包含树形子结构的图数据时存在优化潜力未被发掘。

Method: 1. 进行微实验，在基于关系引擎的图数据库系统中，利用XML数据库开发的结构索引来加速路径查询；2. 提出系统管理树形子结构的愿景，涵盖从建模、模式设计、索引到查询处理的完整生命周期。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过将树形子结构作为一等公民处理，并利用结构索引，可以在基于关系引擎的系统中实现显著的查询加速效果。

Conclusion: 树形子结构应在图数据库系统中获得系统性的原生支持，从建模到查询处理的完整生命周期都应考虑这些结构的特殊性质，这为图数据库研究提出了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Property graphs often contain tree-shaped substructures, yet they are not captured by existing proposals for graph schemas; likewise, query languages and query engines offer little-to-no native support for managing them systematically. As a first contribution, we report on a micro experiment that demonstrates the optimization potential of treating tree-shaped substructures as first class citizens in graph database systems. In particular, we show that in systems backed by relational engines, we can achieve substantial speedups by leveraging structural indexes, as originally developed for XML databases, to accelerate path queries. Based on our findings, we put forward a vision in which tree-shaped substructures are systematically managed throughout the graph query lifecycle, from modeling and schema design to indexing and query processing, and outline arising research questions.

</details>


### [108] [Towards Output-Optimal Uniform Sampling and Approximate Counting for Join-Project Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12560)
*Xiao Hu,Jinchao Huang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文针对连接-投影查询提出了首个渐进最优的采样和计数算法，解决了现有"提议-验证"框架在投影显著减少输出大小时的效率瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接-投影查询在现实数据库应用中普遍存在，但现有采样和计数算法存在效率瓶颈。当投影操作显著减少输出大小时，当前最先进的"提议-验证"框架会产生过高复杂度，无法满足实际需求。

Method: 采用新颖的基于拒绝的采样策略和混合计数归约方法，针对矩阵查询、星型查询和链式查询等基础查询类别设计算法。通过匹配通信复杂度下界证明算法的最优性，并利用代数技术（如快速矩阵乘法）进行分析。

Result: 实现了相对于现有技术的多项式级加速，为矩阵查询和星型查询提供了高效的亚线性时间算法。同时证明了链式查询存在更强的下界，表明亚线性算法在一般情况下是不可能的。

Conclusion: 该研究首次为连接-投影查询提供了渐进最优的采样和计数算法，明确了问题空间的理论边界：矩阵和星型查询支持高效亚线性算法，而链式查询在一般情况下无法实现亚线性算法。

Abstract: Uniform sampling and approximate counting are fundamental primitives for modern database applications, ranging from query optimization to approximate query processing. While recent breakthroughs have established optimal sampling and counting algorithms for full join queries, a significant gap remains for join-project queries, which are ubiquitous in real-world workloads. The state-of-the-art ``propose-and-verify'' framework \cite{chen2020random} for these queries suffers from fundamental inefficiencies, often yielding prohibitive complexity when projections significantly reduce the output size.
  In this paper, we present the first asymptotically optimal algorithms for fundamental classes of join-project queries, including matrix, star, and chain queries. By leveraging a novel rejection-based sampling strategy and a hybrid counting reduction, we achieve polynomial speedups over the state of the art. We establish the optimality of our results through matching communication complexity lower bounds, which hold even against algebraic techniques like fast matrix multiplication. Finally, we delineate the theoretical limits of the problem space. While matrix and star queries admit efficient sublinear-time algorithms, we establish a significantly stronger lower bound for chain queries, demonstrating that sublinear algorithms are impossible in general.

</details>


### [109] [RNSG: A Range-Aware Graph Index for Efficient Range-Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12913)
*Zhiqiu Zou,Ziqi Yin,Rong-Hua Li,Hongchao Qin,Qiangqiang Dai,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种新的范围感知相对邻域图（RRNG）理论，并基于此开发了RNSG索引，用于高效的范围过滤近似最近邻搜索，相比现有方法具有更高的查询性能、更紧凑的索引和更低的构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFANN搜索方法通常需要构建多个范围特定的图索引以实现高查询性能，这带来了显著的索引开销。需要一种既能保证查询性能又能减少索引开销的解决方案。

Method: 首先建立了范围感知相对邻域图（RRNG）理论，证明了其单调搜索能力和结构遗传性。基于此理论提出了RNSG作为RRNG的实用近似，开发了RNSG索引构建算法和RFANN查询处理算法。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的实验表明，RNSG相比现有最先进方法实现了显著更高的查询性能，同时具有更紧凑的索引和更低的构建成本。

Conclusion: 提出的RRNG理论和RNSG索引为RFANN搜索提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案，通过单一图索引实现了高性能的范围过滤最近邻搜索，解决了现有方法的多索引开销问题。

Abstract: Range-filtered approximate nearest neighbor (RFANN) search is a fundamental operation in modern data systems. Given a set of objects, each with a vector and a numerical attribute, an RFANN query retrieves the nearest neighbors to a query vector among those objects whose numerical attributes fall within the range specified by the query. Existing state-of-the-art methods for RFANN search often require constructing multiple range-specific graph indexes to achieve high query performance, which incurs significant indexing overhead. To address this, we first establish a novel graph indexing theory, the range-aware relative neighborhood graph (RRNG), which jointly considers spatial and attribute proximity. We prove that the RRNG satisfies two crucial properties: (1) monotonic search-ability, which ensures correct nearest neighbor retrieval via beam search; and (2) structural heredity, which guarantees that any range-induced subgraph remains a valid RRNG, thus enabling efficient search with a single graph index. Based on this theoretical foundation, we propose a new graph index called RNSG as a practical solution that efficiently approximates RRNG. We develop fast algorithms for both constructing the RNSG index and processing RFANN queries with it. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets show that RNSG achieves significantly higher query performance with a more compact index and lower construction cost than existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [GPU-Accelerated Genetic Programming for Symbolic Regression with Beagle Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12292)
*Nathan Haut,Ilya Basin,Marzieh Kianinejad,Ruchika Gupta,Elijah Smith,Zachary Perrico,Wolfgang Banzhaf*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: Beagle是一个新的GPU软件框架，用于执行遗传编程任务，在符号回归方面显著优于领先的CPU框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个能够在GPU上高效执行遗传编程任务的软件框架，利用GPU的并行计算能力加速符号回归任务，解决现有CPU系统在处理大规模种群和训练样本时的性能瓶颈。

Method: Beagle采用GPU加速的遗传编程框架，通过并行处理种群个体和训练样本来最大化GPU平台的吞吐量。研究使用了两种不同的适应度函数：点对点误差函数和相关适应度函数，并在Feynman符号回归数据集上进行基准测试，与StackGP和PySR等CPU系统在相同时间预算下进行性能比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，Beagle的GPU辅助符号回归系统显著优于领先的基于CPU的框架。在相同的挂钟时间预算下，Beagle在Feynman符号回归数据集上的性能超过了StackGP和PySR系统。

Conclusion: Beagle作为一个新的GPU软件框架，在遗传编程任务特别是符号回归方面表现出色，其GPU加速方法能够显著提升计算性能，为大规模符号回归问题提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Beagle is a new software framework that enables execution of Genetic Programming tasks on the GPU. Currently available for symbolic regression, it processes individuals of the population and fitness cases for training in a way that maximizes throughput on extant GPU platforms. In this contribution, we report on the benchmarking of Beagle on the Feynman Symbolic Regression dataset and compare its performance with a fast CPU system called StackGP and the widely available PySR system under the same wall clock budget. We also report on the use of two different fitness functions, one a point-to-point error function, the other a correlation fitness function. The results demonstrate that the Beagle's GPU-aided Symbolic Regression significantly outperforms leading CPU-based frameworks.

</details>


### [111] [SRAM-Based Compute-in-Memory Accelerator for Linear-decay Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12739)
*Hongyang Shang,Shuai Dong,Yahan Yang,Junyi Yang,Peng Zhou,Arindam Basu*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出基于SRAM的存内计算SNN架构，通过线性衰减近似和并行状态更新方案，解决SNN中神经元膜电位串行更新的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN具有事件驱动和能效高的优势，但其吞吐量受限于神经元膜电位的串行更新。虽然许多硬件加速器和存内计算架构能高效并行化突触操作（W×I），但后续的状态更新步骤仍需O(N)时间来刷新所有神经元膜电位，成为SNN推理中的主要延迟和能耗瓶颈。

Method: 1. 算法层面：用线性衰减近似替代传统的指数膜衰减，将昂贵的乘法运算转换为简单的加法运算，精度损失仅约1%。2. 架构层面：引入存内并行更新方案，直接在SRAM阵列内执行原地衰减，消除全局顺序更新的需求。

Result: 在基准SNN工作负载上评估，所提方法实现了1.1倍到16.7倍的SOP能耗降低，同时提供15.9倍到69倍的能效提升，相对于原始衰减模型的精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 这项工作强调，除了加速（W×I）计算外，在存内计算架构中优化状态更新动态对于实现可扩展、低功耗和实时神经形态处理至关重要。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have emerged as a biologically inspired alternative to conventional deep networks, offering event-driven and energy-efficient computation. However, their throughput remains constrained by the serial update of neuron membrane states. While many hardware accelerators and Compute-in-Memory (CIM) architectures efficiently parallelize the synaptic operation (W x I) achieving O(1) complexity for matrix-vector multiplication, the subsequent state update step still requires O(N) time to refresh all neuron membrane potentials. This mismatch makes state update the dominant latency and energy bottleneck in SNN inference. To address this challenge, we propose an SRAM-based CIM for SNN with Linear Decay Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LD-LIF) Neuron that co-optimizes algorithm and hardware. At the algorithmic level, we replace the conventional exponential membrane decay with a linear decay approximation, converting costly multiplications into simple additions while accuracy drops only around 1%. At the architectural level, we introduce an in-memory parallel update scheme that performs in-place decay directly within the SRAM array, eliminating the need for global sequential updates. Evaluated on benchmark SNN workloads, the proposed method achieves a 1.1 x to 16.7 x reduction of SOP energy consumption, while providing 15.9 x to 69 x more energy efficiency, with negligible accuracy loss relative to original decay models. This work highlights that beyond accelerating the (W x I) computation, optimizing state-update dynamics within CIM architectures is essential for scalable, low-power, and real-time neuromorphic processing.

</details>


### [112] [Federated Few-Shot Learning on Neuromorphic Hardware: An Empirical Study Across Physical Edge Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13037)
*Steven Motta,Gioele Nanni*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该研究探索了神经形态硬件上的联邦学习，发现神经元级连接策略（FedUnion）优于权重平均策略（FedAvg），特征质量是影响准确性的主要因素，特征维度扩展可提升联邦学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态硬件上的脉冲时序依赖可塑性（STDP）产生二进制权重更新，而非标准联邦学习算法所需的浮点梯度，这使得神经形态硬件上的联邦学习尚未被探索。研究旨在解决这一技术挑战。

Method: 构建了一个双节点联邦系统，使用BrainChip Akida AKD1000处理器，进行了约1580次实验试验，测试了四种权重交换策略，重点关注神经元级连接（FedUnion）与元素级权重平均（FedAvg）的比较。

Result: FedUnion策略能持续保持准确性，而FedAvg会破坏准确性（p=0.002）。上游特征提取器的领域自适应微调贡献了大部分准确性提升。将特征维度从64扩展到256可获得77.0%的最佳策略联邦准确性（n=30，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 神经形态硬件上的联邦学习可行，但需要专门的权重交换策略。特征质量是决定性因素，特征维度扩展和神经元原型互补机制是提升性能的关键。跨节点传输的规模与神经元原型的独特性相关。

Abstract: Federated learning on neuromorphic hardware remains unexplored because on-chip spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) produces binary weight updates rather than the floating-point gradients assumed by standard algorithms. We build a two-node federated system with BrainChip Akida AKD1000 processors and run approximately 1,580 experimental trials across seven analysis phases. Of four weight-exchange strategies tested, neuron-level concatenation (FedUnion) consistently preserves accuracy while element-wise weight averaging (FedAvg) destroys it (p = 0.002). Domain-adaptive fine-tuning of the upstream feature extractor accounts for most of the accuracy gains, confirming feature quality as the dominant factor. Scaling feature dimensionality from 64 to 256 yields 77.0% best-strategy federated accuracy (n=30, p < 0.001). Two independent asymmetries (wider features help federation more than individual learning, while binarization hurts federation more) point to a shared prototype complementarity mechanism: cross-node transfer scales with the distinctiveness of neuron prototypes.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [RadEar: A Self-Supervised RF Backscatter System for Voice Eavesdropping and Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12446)
*Qijun Wang,Peihao Yan,Chunqi Qian,Huacheng Zeng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: RadEar是一个基于RF反向散射的隐蔽语音窃听系统，能够在穿墙条件下恢复和分离人类语音


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音窃听对个人隐私和信息安全构成日益增长的威胁，需要开发能够穿透墙壁的隐蔽窃听技术

Method: 系统由两部分组成：1) 部署在目标空间内的无电池RF反向散射标签，采用紧凑双谐振器设计实现高效频率调制；2) 位于室外的RF读取器，执行信号解调、语音分离和降噪，使用基于自监督学习的模型进行语音分离和降噪

Result: 在实际场景中验证了RadEar能够恢复和分离人类语音，在高保真度下工作，并在实际约束条件下表现良好

Conclusion: RadEar系统展示了RF反向散射技术在隐蔽语音窃听方面的潜力，能够有效穿透墙壁并分离重叠语音，对隐私安全构成新的威胁

Abstract: Eavesdropping on voice conversations presents a growing threat to personal privacy and information security. In this paper, we present RadEar, a novel RF backscatter-based system designed to enable covert voice eavesdropping through walls. RadEar consists of two key components: (i) a batteryless RF backscatter tag covertly deployed inside the target space, and (ii) an RF reader located outside the room that performs signal demodulation, voice separation, and denoising. The tag features a compact, dual-resonator design that achieves energy-efficient frequency modulation for continuous voice eavesdropping while mitigating self-interference by separating excitation and reflection frequencies. To overcome the challenges of weak signal reception and overlapping speech, the RF reader employs self-supervised learning models for voice separation and denoising, trained using a remix-based objective without requiring ground-truth labels. We fabricate and evaluate RadEar in real-world scenarios, demonstrating its ability to recover and separate human speech with high fidelity under practical constraints.

</details>


### [114] [Evaluation of TCP Congestion Control for Public High-Performance Wide-Area Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12660)
*Fatih Berkay Sarpkaya,Andrea Francini,Bilgehan Erman,Shivendra Panwar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 评估三种TCP拥塞控制算法（CUBIC、BBRv1、BBRv3）在公共高性能广域网中大数据传输的性能，发现BBRv1结合流量整形能提供最佳的流完成时间预测性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和AI应用越来越多地使用高性能广域网传输海量数据集，准确预测流完成时间对资源调度至关重要。在公共HP-WAN中，由于对关键数据路径参数的控制有限，无法使用RoCEv2，因此需要评估TCP算法的可行性

Method: 在FABRIC测试平台上进行大量实验，评估CUBIC、BBRv1和BBRv3三种TCP拥塞控制算法在公共HP-WAN环境中的性能，特别关注微突发引起的丢包问题，并测试BBRv1结合流量整形技术的效果

Result: 实验表明，在公共HP-WAN的挑战性条件下，配置控制限制会阻碍TCP性能，特别是微突发引起的丢包问题。BBRv1结合流量整形在HP-WAN入口点前应用，能够实现最高的流完成时间预测性

Conclusion: 在公共HP-WAN中，由于无法使用RoCEv2，BBRv1配合流量整形是最佳选择，能够提供最可靠的流完成时间预测，这对于需要精确资源调度的科学和AI应用至关重要

Abstract: Practitioners of a growing number of scientific and artificial-intelligence (AI) applications use High-Performance Wide-Area Networks (HP-WANs) for moving massive data sets between remote facilities. Accurate prediction of the flow completion time (FCT) is essential in these data-transfer workflows because compute and storage resources are tightly scheduled and expensive. We assess the viability of three TCP congestion control algorithms (CUBIC, BBRv1, and BBRv3) for massive data transfers over public HP-WANs, where limited control of critical data-path parameters precludes the use of Remote Direct Memory Access (RDMA) over Converged Ethernet (RoCEv2), which is known to outperform TCP in private HP-WANs. Extensive experiments on the FABRIC testbed indicate that the configuration control limitations can also hinder TCP, especially through microburst-induced packet losses. Under these challenging conditions, we show that the highest FCT predictability is achieved by combination of BBRv1 with the application of traffic shaping before the HP-WAN entry points.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [The Perfection Paradox: From Architect to Curator in AI-Assisted API Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12475)
*Mak Ahmad,Andrew Macvean,JJ Geewax,David Karger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI辅助API设计工作流在工业案例研究中表现出色，但专家们将AI生成的"完美"设计误认为人类作品，揭示了"完美悖论"现象


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决企业API设计中快速功能交付与严格可用性标准维护之间的紧张关系，探索AI辅助设计工作流的实际效果

Method: 基于API改进提案(AIPs)训练的AI辅助设计工作流，通过包含16名行业专家的对照研究，比较AI生成与人类编写的API规范

Result: 定量结果显示AI在11个可用性维度中的10个表现更优，编写时间减少87%；但定性分析发现专家经常误认AI作品为人类作品(准确率仅19%)，并描述这些设计"完美"得令人不安

Conclusion: 提出了"完美悖论"概念，即超一致性暗示缺乏实用的人类判断，建议将人类设计师角色从规范"起草者"转变为AI生成模式的"策展人"

Abstract: Enterprise API design is often bottlenecked by the tension between rapid feature delivery and the rigorous maintenance of usability standards. We present an industrial case study evaluating an AI-assisted design workflow trained on API Improvement Proposals (AIPs). Through a controlled study with 16 industry experts, we compared AI-generated API specifications against human-authored ones. While quantitative results indicated AI superiority in 10 of 11 usability dimensions and an 87% reduction in authoring time, qualitative analysis revealed a paradox: experts frequently misidentified AI work as human (19% accuracy) yet described the designs as unsettlingly "perfect." We characterize this as a "Perfection Paradox" -- where hyper-consistency signals a lack of pragmatic human judgment. We discuss the implications of this perfection paradox, proposing a shift in the human designer's role from the "drafter" of specifications to the "curator" of AI-generated patterns.

</details>


### [116] [How Fair is Software Fairness Testing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12511)
*Ann Barcomb,Mariana Pinheiro Bento,Giuseppe Destefanis,Sherlock Licorish,Cleyton Magalhães,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文批判性地审视了软件公平性测试，指出其文化局限性，主张从普遍性指标转向尊重文化多样性的评估框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件公平性测试将公平性视为固定和普遍适用的概念，忽视了文化背景的差异，导致评估框架存在系统性偏见

Method: 从三个维度分析公平性测试的文化局限性：1) 公平性指标编码特定文化价值观；2) 测试数据集主要基于西方语境；3) 公平性测试引发的伦理问题

Result: 揭示了公平性测试在文化代表性、知识系统包容性和伦理影响方面的系统性缺陷，特别是对全球南方、原住民社区和非数字社区的边缘化

Conclusion: 需要重新思考公平性测试，超越普遍性指标，建立尊重文化多元性、承认拒绝算法调解权利的评估框架

Abstract: Software fairness testing is a central method for evaluating AI systems, yet the meaning of fairness is often treated as fixed and universally applicable. This vision paper positions fairness testing as culturally situated and examines the problem across three dimensions. First, fairness metrics encode particular cultural values while marginalizing others. Second, test datasets are predominantly designed from Western contexts, excluding knowledge systems grounded in oral traditions, Indigenous languages, and non-digital communities. Third, fairness testing raises ethical concerns, including the reliance on low-paid data labeling in the Global South, and associated with this, the environmental costs of training and deploying large-scale models, which disproportionately affect climate-vulnerable populations. Addressing these issues requires rethinking fairness testing beyond universal metrics and moving toward evaluation frameworks that respect cultural plurality and acknowledge the right to refuse algorithmic mediation.

</details>


### [117] [Linguistic Similarity Within Centralized FLOSS Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12571)
*Matthew Gaughan,Aaron Shaw,Darren Gergle*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究开源软件中基金会主导的开发是否影响贡献者讨论风格，发现尽管基金会主导开发，但基金会成员与外部贡献者的语言风格没有差异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自由/开源软件中基金会等管理者集中化开发对项目可持续性和贡献者讨论方式的影响，特别是基金会主导开发是否会导致讨论中的层级语言差异

Method: 采用混合方法多案例比较，分析三个维基媒体平台功能在MediaWiki中的开发，使用仓库挖掘、语言风格特征和主成分分析来追踪功能开发和问题讨论

Result: 与直觉和先前研究相反，维基媒体基金会成员和外部贡献者的语言风格没有可识别的差异，即使功能开发由基金会贡献指导

Conclusion: 提出两个对协作开源软件开发研究的启示：1)管理者根据自身对特定功能的使用主导开发；2)集中化项目开发不必然导致项目讨论中的层级语言

Abstract: When free/libre and open source software (FLOSS) stewards centralize project development, they potentially undermine project sustainability and impact how contributors talk to each other. To study the relationship between steward-centralized development and contributor discussion, we compared the development of three Wikimedia platform features that the Wikimedia Foundation (WMF) built in MediaWiki. In a mixed-methods multi-case comparison, we used repository mining, linguistic style features, and principal component analysis to track MediaWiki feature development and issue discussions. Contrary to both our intuition and prior work, there were no identifiable differences in the linguistic style of WMF-affiliates and external contributors, even when feature development was guided by WMF contributions. From these results, we offer two provocations to the study of collaborative FLOSS development: (1) stewards dominate development according to their own use of specific project functionality; (2) centralized project development does not entail hierarchical language within project discussions.

</details>


### [118] [Design-Specification Tiling for ICL-based CAD Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12712)
*Yali Du,San-Zhuo Xi,Hui Sun,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Design-Specification Tiling (DST)方法，通过知识充分性目标改进CAD代码生成的示例选择策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码生成方面表现出色，但在CAD等特定领域任务中因训练数据稀缺而表现不佳。现有上下文学习中的示例选择策略侧重于相似性或点式多样性，无法满足复杂CAD设计规范中的组合需求。

Method: 提出知识充分性作为示例选择的原则性目标，旨在最大程度满足设计规范中的所有需求。引入Design-Specification Tiling (DST)方法，通过提取多粒度设计组件并测量查询组件被选定示例覆盖的比例来量化知识充分性。

Result: DST显著提高了CAD代码生成质量，在上下文学习中始终优于现有的示例选择策略。证明了最大化该目标构成子模最大化，并提供了具有(1-1/e)近似保证的多项式时间贪婪算法。

Conclusion: DST通过知识充分性目标改进了CAD代码生成的示例选择，解决了现有策略在满足复杂设计规范组合需求方面的不足，为领域特定代码生成提供了有效的训练免费解决方案。
